### [1] [Steady state oscillations of a half cylinder with a pseudo isotropic structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07612)
*Mario Argueta Garcia*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了半无限圆柱体在伪各向同性弹性结构下振荡问题中，通过变量分离得到的某类自伴二次束的谱性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于半无限圆柱体具有伪各向同性弹性结构时的振荡问题，通过变量分离方法可以导出相应的自伴二次束，理解其谱性质对于解决原始物理问题至关重要。

Method: 利用变量分离技术将半无限圆柱体的弹性振荡问题转化为对特定自伴二次束的谱分析。

Result: 获得了该类自伴二次束的谱性质的相关结论（具体结果需查阅全文）。

Conclusion: 关于伪各向同性弹性半无限圆柱体振荡问题中分离变量后产生的自伴二次束，其谱性质已在本文中得到研究。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate the spectral properties of certain self-adjoint quadratic pencils, occurring at the separation of the variables in the problem of the oscillation of a semi-infinite cylinder with a pseudo isotropic elastic structure.

</details>


### [2] [An isoperimetric inequality for Neumann eigenvalues with radial log-concave measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08748)
*Kui Wang,Xiaomei Sun,Anqiang Zhu*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了空间形式中具有径向对数凹测度的原点对称Lipschitz域上，Witten-Laplacian前n个非零Neumann特征值的调和平均的锐利等周不等式。主要创新点在于在没有假设权函数非递增的一般径向对数凹测度下建立了该不等式，推广了先前局限于特定或更受限加权设置的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展先前仅在特定或更受限加权条件下成立的Witten-Laplacian特征值等周不等式，使其适用于一般径向对数凹测度，特别是去除了权函数非递增的假设限制。

Method: 证明依赖于三个关键技术：1）对测地球上第一特征函数的精细分析；2）由径向权重的凸性条件导出的单调性性质；3）矩阵迹不等式。

Result: 在一般径向对数凹测度下，建立了空间形式中原点对称Lipschitz域上Witten-Laplacian前n个非零Neumann特征值调和平均的锐利等周不等式，推广了已有的加权特征值不等式结果。

Conclusion: 该结果在更广泛的测度框架下建立了Witten-Laplacian特征值的尖锐几何不等式，为谱几何与加权测度交叉领域提供了新的一般性工具和理论贡献。

Abstract: We prove a sharp isoperimetric inequality for the harmonic mean of the first $n$ nonzero Neumann eigenvalues of the Witten-Laplacian on origin-symmetric Lipschitz domains in space forms, endowed with radial log-concave measures. The main novelty is that we establish the sharp harmonic mean inequality under general radial log-concave measures, without assuming the weight function to be non-increasing. This extends previous results that were restricted to specific or more restrictive weighted settings. The proof relies on a refined analysis of the first eigenfunction on geodesic balls, a monotonicity property derived from a convexity condition on the radial weight, and a matrix trace inequality.

</details>


### [3] [Two escape rates for negative eigenvalues of quantum graphs with a shrinking core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08946)
*Gregory Berkolaiko,Denis Borisov,Marshall King,Julien Royer*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有一般顶点匹配条件和两种长度尺度的度量图上拉普拉斯算子的负谱。当小尺度参数ε→0时，部分负特征值以ε⁻¹和ε⁻²/³两种速率逃逸至负无穷，作者精确描述了逃逸速率、特征值数量及其首项系数，并基于图的Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射和隐式Rellich型定理给出完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量图上的谱分析是量子图理论的核心问题之一。当图包含多尺度结构（紧凑核心与无限长边）时，负特征值的渐近行为在奇摄动和均匀化问题中具有重要物理意义，但此前缺乏对一般顶点条件下不同逃逸速率的系统刻画。

Method: 作者利用图的Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射将问题转化为非线性2参数矩阵束的分析，并借助一个隐式Rellich型定理，将矩阵束解分支的幂律速率与单参数Hermitian族的前导阶联系起来，从而精确刻画负特征值在ε→0时的渐近行为。

Result: 确定了负特征值恰好以两种速率逃逸：快速率ε⁻¹和分数速率ε⁻²/³；对每种速率的特征值数量及其首项系数都从顶点条件中显式给出。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了双尺度度量图上拉普拉斯负谱的奇异渐近，揭示了分数阶逃逸速率ε⁻²/³这一非平凡现象，并为多尺度量子图的谱理论提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the negative spectrum of the Laplacian on a metric graph with general vertex matching conditions and with two length scales: a compact core whose edges have length of order a small parameter $ε$, together with finitely many edges of infinite length. As $ε\to0$, some negative eigenvalues may escape to $-\infty$, and we describe precisely how. There are exactly two rates of escape, $ε^{-1}$ and the fractional rate $ε^{-2/3}$. We determine the number of eigenvalues of each rate, together with their leading coefficients, explicitly from the vertex conditions. The analysis rests on the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map of the graph and on an implicit Rellich-type theorem, that identifies the power-law rates of the solution branches of a nonlinear 2-parameter matrix pencil with the leading orders of a one-parameter Hermitian family.

</details>


### [4] [Eigenvalues of the Magnetic Neumann Laplacian on Domains with Peaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09469)
*Noah Koerner*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维有界尖峰域上磁Neumann Laplacian在大磁场参数λ趋于+∞时的特征值渐近行为，证明其主项阶数为λ^(2/(q+1))，且受尖峰锐度q和几何形状影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有关于光滑域和带角域上磁Neumann Laplacian特征值渐近的研究，本文将其推广到具有向外尖峰（outward peaks）的边界几何情形，探究尖峰尖锐程度对特征值渐近展开的影响。

Method: 通过考虑磁Neumann Laplacian在带尖峰的有界域上的谱问题，引入与参数λ相关的大磁场，利用渐近分析（可能结合局部坐标变换、边界层方法和谱渐近估计技术）推导特征值在λ→+∞时的展开式及其主项量级。

Result: 证明了特征值的渐近展开受尖峰的锐度参数q和几何结构影响，特征值主项阶数为λ^(2/(q+1))，该结果覆盖并推广了光滑域和角域情形的已有结论。

Conclusion: 尖峰域的几何奇异性（锐度q）显著改变磁Neumann Laplacian特征值的阶数，本文为理解非光滑边界与磁场相互作用的谱理论提供了新的推广结果。

Abstract: We consider the magnetic Neumann Laplacian on bounded domains in $\mathbb{R}^2$ with outward peaks. The operator is associated with a large magnetic field depending on a parameter $λ$, and we investigate the behaviour of the eigenvalues as $λ$ tends to $+\infty$. We show that their asymptotic expansion is influenced by the sharpness $q$ and geometry of the peak, and that its main term is of order $λ^{\frac{2}{q+1}}$. This is an extension of previous works on magnetic Neumann Laplacians in smooth domains and domains with corners.

</details>


### [5] [Spherical n-lunes: billiards and eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09557)
*Pedro Freitas,Isabel Salavessa*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了球面月牙形区域上测地线台球（geodesic billiards）的周期轨道，确定了当角度开口为π/p形式时Dirichlet和Neumann谱，并证明了角度小于π且非π有理倍数的月牙，或角度为π/p（p>1）的月牙最终满足Pólya猜想，同时给出了特征值的两项渐近公式及计数函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面上的测地线台球系统是将经典平面台球理论推广到高维球面几何中的重要课题。理解这类系统的周期轨道性质及其与谱、Pólya猜想的关系，对谱几何和动力系统交叉领域具有理论意义。

Method: 通过球面几何中月牙区域的对称性，利用可积系统/分离变量方法确定周期轨道并计算Dirichlet与Neumann谱；结合测地线台球的动力系统方法建立计数函数，并利用估计技术证明Pólya猜想的最终成立性及特征值的两项渐近公式，最后给出了维度相关的显式界p。

Result: 1) 完全确定了角度为π/p的球面月牙的Dirichlet和Neumann谱；2) 证明了角度小于π且非π有理倍数，以及角度为π/p（p>1）的月牙最终满足Pólya猜想；3) 对π/p角度的月牙给出了基于尖锐上下界的特征值两项渐近公式及相应的两项计数函数；4) 给出了保证Pólya猜想对所有特征值成立的p的维度相关显式界。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地刻画了球面月牙上测地线台球的周期轨道与谱结构，将Pólya猜想的验证推广到一类具有特殊对称性的球面区域，并提供了精确的渐近估计和计数函数，为高维球面台球系统的谱理论提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We characterise the periodic orbits of geodesic billiards on spherical lunes on $\mathbb{S}^{n}$. In the case of angle openings of the form $π/p$ for positive integer $p$ we fully determine their Dirichlet and Neumann spectra. We then show that lunes with an angle opening smaller than $π$ which is not a rational multiple of $π$, or those with an angle opening of the form $π/p$ for $p$ larger than one satisfy Pólya's conjecture eventually, independently of whether the corresponding geodesic billiards satisfy the nonperiodicity condition or not. For lunes with an angle opening $π/p$ we further provide a two-term asymptotic formula for the eigenvalues based on sharp upper and lower bounds, together with a corresponding two-term counting function established using the geoesic billiards approach. Finally,we give an explicit bound on $p$ in terms of the dimension ensuring the corresponding lunes satisfy Pólya's conjecture for all eigenvalues.

</details>


### [6] [A remark on Weyl-type bounds for Steklov eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09632)
*Luigi Provenzano*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一个否定性结果：对于$n\geq 3$，不存在仅依赖于$n$的常数$C_n$，使得所有光滑有界域$\Omega\subset\mathbb R^n$的Steklov特征值$\sigma_k(\Omega)$满足上界估计$\sigma_k(\Omega)\leq C_n\left(\frac{k}{|\partial\Omega|}\right)^{\frac{1}{n-1}}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Steklov特征值问题中，经典的Weyl渐近公式表明特征值$\sigma_k$大致以$\left(\frac{k}{|\partial\Omega|}\right)^{\frac{1}{n-1}}$的速率增长。一个自然的问题是：是否存在一个仅依赖于维数$n$的普适常数$C_n$，使得该渐近上界对所有光滑有界域一致成立（即所谓的Shapiro猜想或类似的一致上界估计）？作者旨在否定这一猜想。

Method: 通过构造一类特殊的光滑有界域序列，使得其Steklov特征值$\sigma_k$的增长速率超过$(k/|\partial\Omega|)^{1/(n-1)}$，从而证明不存在普适常数$C_n$能满足所有域的一致性上界。利用维度$n\geq 3$的特殊几何构造（可能与高维空间的复杂拓扑或细薄结构有关）来放大特征值。

Result: 证明了对于$n\geq 3$，上述形式的Steklov特征值一致上界估计不成立，即不存在仅依赖$n$的常数$C_n$。

Conclusion: 该结果否定了Steklov特征值问题中可能存在的一致Weyl型上界估计，表明在$n\geq 3$维空间中，Steklov特征值可以以比标准Weyl速率更快的速度增长，这与$n=2$情形（已知存在类似上界）形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: We prove that, for $n\geq 3$, there is no constant $C_n$ depending only on $n$ such that the Steklov eigenvalues $σ_k(Ω)$ satisfy $σ_k(Ω)\leq C_n\left(\frac{k}{|\partialΩ|}\right)^{\frac1{n-1}}$ for every smooth bounded domain $Ω\subset\mathbb R^n$ and every $k\geq1 $.

</details>

### [7] [Novel Dynamics in Models of Angiogenesis with p-Laplacian diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07675)
*Wenbo Zhang,Hossein Asgaribakhtiari,Aishwarya Pawar,Rana D. Parshad*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个经典的血管生成（angiogenesis）双物种模型（细胞与VEGF群体），其中细胞运动遵循p-Laplacian扩散（涵盖快扩散1<p<2、慢扩散p>2及正常扩散p=2）。作者证明了当p>3/2且初始数据足够小时系统在弱意义下适定，并发现p-Laplacian可引发多种此前未报道的新动力学行为，包括双模态及多尖峰解导致的细胞增殖增强、正则性增益、有限时间爆破的抑制、有限时间灭绝导致的细胞耗竭以及图灵斑图。最后讨论了这些结果在心脏健康数字孪生框架中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺血性心脏病是全球死亡的主要原因，血运重建（恢复堵塞血管血流）是重要治疗手段。血管生成的数学模型已被广泛研究，但传统的双物种模型通常假设正常扩散（p=2）。作者引入更一般的p-Laplacian扩散来更真实地刻画细胞运动，探索其带来的新动力学行为，并服务于心脏健康的数字孪生应用。

Method: 采用经典的双物种血管生成模型（细胞与VEGF群体），将细胞扩散项推广为p-Laplacian扩散算子，涵盖快扩散（1<p<2）、慢扩散（p>2）和正常扩散（p=2）三种情形。通过弱解的理论分析（当p>3/2且初始数据足够小时证明系统适定性），并结合数值模拟识别p-Laplacian引入的新型动力学现象。

Result: (1) 证明了当p>3/2且初始数据足够小时，系统在弱意义下是适定的；(2) 发现了p-Laplacian扩散导致的多种新现象：双模态和多尖峰解带来的细胞增殖增强、正则性增益、有限时间爆破的抑制、有限时间灭绝引起的细胞耗竭、以及图灵斑图的形成；(3) 将上述结果应用于心脏健康的数字孪生框架。

Conclusion: p-Laplacian扩散为血管生成模型提供了更丰富的动力学行为，不仅拓宽了模型的数学适定性范围，还揭示了正常扩散模型中无法观察到的细胞增殖、耗竭和模式形成等现象。这些发现为构建心脏血运重建的数字孪生框架提供了理论支撑，有潜力指导缺血性心脏病的个性化治疗策略。

Abstract: Ischemic heart diseases represent the leading cause of mortality worldwide. Revascularization, the process to restore blood flow in blockages, shows promise. To this end, mathematical models for angiogenesis, the process by which new blood vessels form from existing ones, have been extremely well investigated. In the current work, we consider a classical two species model for angiogenesis, consisting of cell and VEGF populations. However, we assume the cells move according to p-Laplacian diffusion, which could be both ``fast" ($1<p<2$) and ``slow" ($p>2$), in addition to normal diffusion ($p=2$). We first show that the system is well posed in a weak sense when $p>\frac{3}{2}$, for sufficiently small initial data. Next, we show that the p-Laplacian can lead to several novel dynamics not reported earlier; these include increased cellular proliferation via bi-modal and multi spike solutions, gain of regularity, prevention of finite time blow-up, cell depletion via finite time extinction, and Turing patterns. We discuss applications of these results for cardiac health via a digital twins framework.

</details>


### [8] [Large time behavior of the solution to the Cauchy problem for viscous Hamilton-Jacobi equation on infinite graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07682)
*Alan A. Tedeev*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了加权无限图上带梯度吸收的非线性扩散方程的长时间行为。在图的体积多项式增长条件下，建立了总质量衰减的尖锐估计，证明了当吸收指数低于某个依赖于体积增长率和扩散指数的临界阈值时，总质量随时间趋于零。该临界指数与欧氏空间中Fujita型阈值一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散结构（加权图）上结合扩散与非线性阻尼的偏微分方程的长时间渐近行为，填补了该方程在图上的长时间衰减理论空白，并验证离散情形下Fujita型临界指数与连续欧氏空间情形的对应关系。

Method: 结合Hardy不等式与Hölder不等式，引入一个依赖于图几何的时间相关缩放参数，在图的体积多项式增长条件及参数的序关系假设下，推导总质量随时间的衰减估计。

Result: 证明了当吸收指数低于临界阈值时，解的总体质量随时间趋于零；该临界阈值依赖于图的体积增长率与扩散指数，与欧氏空间中的Fujita型临界指数一致。结果对线性扩散情形具新颖性，并适用于整数格和有限生成幂零群的Cayley图等广泛图类。

Conclusion: 该研究为离散图上的非线性扩散-吸收方程建立了尖锐的质量衰减结果，揭示了离散结构与连续设定之间Fujita型临界现象的一致性，并为更一般图类上的相关研究提供了方法基础。

Abstract: We study the long-time behavior of nonnegative solutions to a nonlinear diffusion equation with gradient absorption on infinite weighted graphs. The equation combines a discrete p-Laplacian diffusion term with a nonlinear absorption term of Hamilton-Jacobi type, modeling processes where both diffusion and nonlinear damping occur on discrete structures. Under the assumptions that the graph satisfies a polynomial volume growth condition and that the parameters satisfy certain ordering relations, we establish sharp decay estimates for the total mass of the solution. We prove that the total mass decays to zero as time tends to infinity whenever the absorption exponent lies below a critical threshold. This critical exponent depends explicitly on the volume growth rate of the graph and the diffusion exponent, and coincides with the Fujita-type threshold known for analogous equations in the continuous Euclidean setting. Our proof combines Hardy and Hölder inequalities with a carefully chosen time-dependent scaling parameter adapted to the graph geometry. The results are new even in the linear diffusion case and extend naturally to a broad class of graphs, including integer lattices and Cayley graphs of finitely generated nilpotent groups.

</details>


### [9] [Relative Faber--Krahn inequalities and Trudinger's equation on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07691)
*Philipp Sürig*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在黎曼流形上研究Trudinger方程，证明了相对p-Faber-Krahn不等式等价于体积倍增性质加上非负有界弱次解的次高斯上界估计，并在一致p-Faber-Krahn不等式下推导出改进的长时间上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黎曼流形上研究退化/奇异非线性抛物方程（Trudinger方程）的热核估计与几何-解析不等式之间的等价关系，是几何分析中的核心问题。本文旨在建立p-Faber-Krahn不等式与Trudinger方程解的上界估计之间的等价性，从而深化对这类非线性扩散方程内在结构的理解。

Method: 作者在黎曼流形上考虑Trudinger方程∂_t u = Δ_p u^{1/(p-1)}（p>1），通过分析相对p-Faber-Krahn不等式与体积倍增条件之间的关系，建立了与次高斯上界估计的等价性。同时利用一致p-Faber-Krahn不等式推导改进的长时间上界估计。

Result: 证明了相对p-Faber-Krahn不等式等价于体积倍增条件与非负有界弱次解的次高斯上界估计的合取；在一致p-Faber-Krahn不等式条件下获得了改进的长期时间上界估计。

Conclusion: 本文建立了黎曼流形上Trudinger方程解的上界估计与p-Faber-Krahn不等式之间的精确等价关系，为研究非线性p-Laplace型退化扩散方程的函数不等式与解析估计之间的深层联系提供了新视角。

Abstract: We consider on Riemannian manifolds the Trudinger equation \begin{equation*}\partial _{t}u=Δ_{p}u^{\frac{1}{p-1}},\end{equation*} where $p>1$. We prove that a relative $p$--Faber--Krahn inequality is equivalent to the conjunction of volume doubling and a sub-Gaussian upper estimate for non-negative bounded weak subsolutions of Trudinger's equation. We also derive an improved long-time upper estimate under a uniform $p$--Faber--Krahn inequality.

</details>

Abstract: This paper develops a Hodge-theoretic feedback framework for flow-valued states, motivated by scalar parabolic thermostat problems with localized sensors and dual sources. On a connected graph, the edge space decomposes into a cut space and a harmonic cycle space. The ideal topological thermostat regulates the cut component toward a prescribed cycle-free transfer target and damps the harmonic component. For realizations through selected physical edge channels, the first Betti number gives the minimal sensing and actuation ranks required for exact compression of the full harmonic sector. Minimal rank does not imply local realization of the nonlocal Hodge projector; the remaining cut-harmonic blocks quantify spillover and forcing.
  For transmission networks, we formulate ideal and finite-channel nonlinear line-actuated swing closures and linearize the complete closed systems at an angle-cohesive synchronous equilibrium. With the actuator fixed, passive tangent swing motion preserves any pre-existing harmonic line-flow component. The ideal closure replaces this conservation law by exponential decay and appends a stable harmonic block without changing the reduced passive nodal-cut spectrum. Under the channel-rank conditions, finite realizations reproduce the harmonic compression, whereas autonomous harmonic decay and full reduced-state recovery require additional decoupling and Hurwitz conditions. A target-centred Bregman balance yields nonlinear dissipation for the ideal metric-compatible controller and for canonical colocated finite-channel feedback. Explicit theta-network, microgrid, ring, and synthetic IEEE 14-bus examples illustrate transfer regulation, cycle-flow damping, and localization-induced transients. Realistic device dynamics, constraints, and large-scale validation remain open.

Abstract: We construct, via convex integration, a nonzero singular weak solution of the one-dimensional periodic cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation that is compactly supported in time, has zero initial datum, and satisfies $u\in\bigcap_{α<1/6} C_t^0 H_x^α\cap \bigcap_{1\leq p<3} C_t^0 L_x^p$. For the full cubic NLS, the spatially constant component generated by the nonlinearity cannot be discarded. We overcome this zero-output obstruction with a two-carrier perturbation: its unique zero-carrier, zero-slab-output interaction reproduces the full old error, including its zero Fourier mode, while the remaining interactions are perturbative in a negative Wiener norm. The nonlinear term is defined through a signed absolute-Fourier summability condition and is the common limit generated by every admissible Fourier cutoff. Whenever a singular solution belongs to $C_t^0 L_x^3$, this nonlinear term agrees with the ordinary product $|u|^2u$. Since $H^{1/6}(\mathbb T)\hookrightarrow L^3(\mathbb T)$ and unconditional uniqueness holds in $C_t^0 H_x^{1/6}$ by a result of Guo, Kwon, and Oh, the threshold $1/6$ is sharp within the singular solution class considered here.

Abstract: This paper provides a comprehensive treatment of inverse boundary value problems for (nonlinear) Kirchhoff plate equations under diverse general settings. We begin by establishing the global well-posedness of the nonlinear forward equations, which not only underpins the subsequent inverse analysis but also holds independent theoretical significance. The inverse problems are then examined for both passive and active measurement regimes. With a single passive boundary measurement, we establish the stable recovery of the unknown initial data. In the active regime with infinitely many boundary measurements, our results are twofold. For linear equations featuring generic time-dependent potentials-allowing for spatial unboundedness, we demonstrate the simultaneous recovery of both initial data and coefficients. For nonlinear equations, where both the nonlinearity and initial data are unknown, we develop a novel Runge approximation approach, together with carefully constructed geometric optics solutions and higher-order linearization around nonzero solutions, to prove their simultaneous determination. Furthermore, we introduce a delicate cut-off technique that provides an alternative means of addressing the scenario of vanishing initial data. Notably, the methodologies and results developed herein are readily generalizable to other boundary conditions and plate models, including the classical Euler-Bernoulli equation.

In this setting, we show that the leading asymptotics are entirely determined by the geometry of the underlying Abelian cover, while the nontrivial fiber modes decay exponentially fast due to a uniform spectral gap. Abstract: We study the spatial regularity of regular Lagrangian flows associated with vector fields in the DiPerna--Lions class \(\boldsymbol b\in L^1((0,T);W^{1,1}_{\loc}(\mathbb R^d)), \) under the standard growth and compressibility assumptions. More precisely, we construct an increasing function \(G\), with \(G(0+)=-\infty\), determined by the integrability of \(D\boldsymbol b\), such that \[ G\bigl(|\boldsymbol X(t,x)-\boldsymbol X(t,y)|\bigr) \leq G\bigl(|\boldsymbol X(s,x)-\boldsymbol X(s,y)|\bigr) + \int_s^t \bigl(k(τ,x)+k(τ,y)\bigr)\,\ddτ, \] where \(k\) is locally integrable. As a consequence, the flow \(\boldsymbol X(t,\cdot)\) is uniformly continuous outside a set of arbitrarily small measure, with an explicit modulus of continuity determined by the integrability properties of \(D\boldsymbol b\). The resulting moduli include Hölder and log-Lipschitz regimes, as well as substantially weaker Osgood moduli. Our approach is based on a new family of weighted maximal operators associated with slowly varying functions in the sense of Karamata. We also provide examples showing that the resulting estimates are sharp in several respects and that the classical Lipschitz-type estimate may fail at the endpoint \(p=1\). Finally, we apply the flow estimates to transport equations, obtaining weighted logarithmic Sobolev regularity for transported scalars and corresponding lower bounds on functional and geometric mixing scales in the \(W^{1,1}\) setting.

For alternating fences it gives an extended Fibonacci recurrence and a Grothendieck lift of both Ehrhart terms in the exact Hilbert--Kunz formula for quadrics.
  Finally, an averaged decoration product places the complexes for all compositions in a differential graded Hopf bar. The classical normalization matching is strictly Hopf-compatible, while a rigidity theorem rules out any further nonidentity normalized contraction that preserves the same deconcatenation on the reduced even-block model.

Abstract: Let z(m,n;s,t) be the largest number of ones in an m x n zero-one matrix with no s x t all-ones submatrix. Roman's 1975 inequality remains the best general upper bound for s>=3, but it is not attained on a large part of the range just below the design threshold T=(t-1)C(m,s)/(s+1).
  The proof has two steps. First, for n=T-c with 1<=c<=sT/(s+2), Roman's bound equals the elementary counting bound (s+1)(T-c)+floor(2c/s), adding nothing beyond a budget inequality and convexity. Second, attainment forces all but at most one column to have size s+1 or s+2; each such column has a point lying in a number of s-sets divisible by d=gcd(s,C(s+1,2)), pinning the leftover coverage there to a single residue mu mod d, which a global count rules out. For the refined program of Davies, Gill, and Horsley, its optimum is still attained at the Roman vertex on an explicit sub-family, and we record where their program does better.

</details>

Abstract: This note propose a counterexample to the Anstee--Sali conjecture for forbidden configurations. The basic candidate is the $4$-uniform family

  \] which is asymptotically larger than $m^3$. The example was found by GPT-5.6 Sol.

Abstract: We introduce a new algorithm for computing Kostka-Foulkes polynomials. It arises from a combinatorial procedure that computes the transition coefficients between successive pre-canonical bases for spherical Hecke algebras, introduced by Libedinsky, Patimo, and the first author, in order to interpolate between the standard and canonical bases of the spherical Hecke algebras. Using this algorithm, we prove that the coefficients of the polynomials in the transition matrix from any pre-canonical basis to the next one has nonnegative coefficients. This establishes the positivity conjecture for pre-canonical bases and, moreover, yields a strictly stronger result that uncovers additional positivity phenomena beyond the original scope of the conjecture. As a by-product, this method yields a combinatorial algorithm for computing Lascoux's atomic decomposition of canonical basis elements. Finally, our results show that, after applying the Satake isomorphism and dualizing with respect to the Hall inner product, pre-canonical bases correspond to a new family of Schur-positive symmetric functions.

Method: 提出递归包含不等式的符号消元理论：首先证明每个参数赋值存在唯一最小凸集值解；然后设计有限的高斯消元式过程，在消去未知量的同时保持解不变，生成仅含参数的坐标集表达式。进一步推广到包含空集、对有限并集、非负伸缩、闵可夫斯基和、正几何连接和凸包封闭的子集族B上，证明解坐标集在B中闭包。最后将此理论应用于特定结构的层压包络，推导其半代数性和有效可计算性。

Result: 证明了：(1) 每个参数赋值对递归包含不等式系统有唯一最小凸集值解；(2) 存在有限的高斯消元式程序生成仅含参数的坐标表达式；(3) 当参数属于满足特定闭包性质的子集族B时，解的各坐标集也在B中；特别地，当参数是半面体（有界凸半线性集）的有限并时，每个坐标集也是半面体且有无量词半线性描述；(4) 对V=U⊕⊕W_i、Λ=∪(U+W_i)的结构，有限集S的层压包络G_Λ^(∞)(S)是半代数的，可由实上的无量词公式有效计算。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一类递归包含不等式的完整符号消元理论，与经典线性不等式的高斯消元法形成类比，同时推广到更一般的闭包子集族。该理论不仅能刻画最小解的代数结构，还为层压包络提供了有效的量词消去计算方法，为凸几何与半代数几何的交叉应用提供了新工具。

Abstract: We develop a symbolic elimination theory for finite systems of recursive containment inequalities whose unknowns are convex subsets of a finite-dimensional real vector space. The right-hand sides are formal expressions generated from variables and parameters by convex linear combinations, finite union, and a positive geometric join encoding strict convex combinations. We prove that every parameter assignment has a unique smallest convex-set-valued solution and give a finite Gaussian-elimination-type procedure that eliminates the unknowns while preserving this solution and produces parameter-only expressions for its coordinate sets.
  More generally, let $\mathcal B$ be a family of subsets containing $\emptyset$ and closed under finite unions, nonnegative dilation, Minkowski sums, positive geometric joins, and convex hulls. If all parameter sets lie in $\mathcal B$, then every coordinate set of the smallest solution lies in $\mathcal B$; when these operations are effective, so is the resulting description. In particular, if the parameters are finite unions of hemihedra---where a hemihedron is a bounded convex semi-linear set, equivalently a convex finite union of relative interiors of polytopes---then each coordinate set is a hemihedron and admits a quantifier-free semi-linear description.
  We apply this theory to lamination hulls. For \[ V=U\oplus\bigoplus_{i=1}^{k}W_i,\qquad \dim W_i=1,\qquad Λ=\bigcup_{i=1}^{k}(U+W_i), \] we prove that the lamination hull $G_Λ^{(\infty)}(S)$ of every finite $S\subset V$ is semi-algebraic and effectively computable by a quantifier-free formula over the reals.

</details>

Result: 对于任意正整数n，构造出了满足χ(G),χ(H) > n且χ(G×H) = 4的有限简单图对(G, H)，表明张量积的色数可以远小于两个因子图的色数。

Conclusion: 该结果展示了张量积色数的极端压缩现象：两个高色数图的张量积色数可以低至4，这与Hedetniemi猜想所暗示的"乘积色数等于较小色数"的直觉形成鲜明对比，为理解张量积色数的边界行为提供了重要构造。

Abstract: For any $n$, there exists finite simple graphs $G$ and $H$ such that $χ(G),χ(H) > n$ and $χ(G \times H) = 4$.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图多项式的分解通常反映组合结构的分解。为了建立交错多项式与Tutte多项式之间在代数不可约性层面的平行理论，需要系统地刻画交错多项式的因子分解条件及其与图连通性的关系。

Method: 通过代数与图论相结合的方法，利用多项式环上的不可约性判定理论，分别对双变量交错多项式q(G;x,y)和Courcelle多元交错多项式C_G(u,v;x,y)进行分析，并构造反例族以说明无环条件的必要性。

Conclusion: 交错多项式的不可约性精确刻画了图的连通性，与Tutte多项式的经典结论形成完美平行；同时揭示了无环条件在双变量交错多项式情形下的必要性，而Courcelle多元版本则无需此条件。

Abstract: The factorisation of graph polynomials often reflects combinatorial decomposition. For a nonempty loopless graph $G$, we first prove that the two-variable interlace polynomial $q(G;x,y)$, introduced by Arratia, Bollobás and Sorkin, is irreducible over $\mathbb{C}[x,y]$ if and only if $G$ is connected, exactly paralleling the classical irreducibility theorem for the Tutte polynomial. The loopless hypothesis is essential: we construct an infinite family of connected looped graphs whose two-variable interlace polynomials are reducible. For a nonempty graph $G$, we prove that Courcelle's multivariate interlace polynomial $C_G(u,v;\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})$ is irreducible over $\mathbb{C}[u,v,x_a,y_a:a\in V(G)]$ if and only if $G$ is connected.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了Brown-Mol提出的猜想，并给出了子树根分布的上下界及其精确性分析，为子树多项式根分布理论提供了完整的刻画。两个界在适当条件下均表现出最优性或渐近最优性，表明该结果是几乎不可改进的。

Abstract: The subtree polynomial of a tree is the generating function that enumerates its subtrees according to their orders. Brown and Mol conjectured that every subtree root of a tree of order \(n\ge 2\) lies in the disk \[ \left\{z\in\mathbb C: |z|\le 1+\sqrt[n-1]{n-1} \right\}. \] We confirm this conjecture. We also show that every nonzero subtree root \(z\) satisfies \[ |z|>\sqrt[n-1]{n-1}-1. \] Consequently, all nonzero subtree roots lie in the annular region
  \[ \left\{z\in\mathbb C: \sqrt[n-1]{n-1}-1 <
|z| \le 1+\sqrt[n-1]{n-1} \right\}. \] The upper bound is sharp for every even \(n\) and is asymptotically sharp as \(n\to\infty\) through odd integers. The lower bound is also asymptotically sharp as \(n\to\infty\).

Abstract: For $d\ge 1$, let $L(d)\in\mathbb N\cup\{\infty\}$ be the supremum of the lengths of paths in $\mathbb Z^d$ whose steps are standard basis vectors and whose vertex sets contain no collinear triple. We prove that \[

Abstract: We analyse topology of random simplicial complexes in the medial regime. We show that these complexes are highly connected and have homotopy type of iterated suspensions. One of our main tools is a new combinatorial criterion for high connectivity of simplicial complexes, which is more flexible than conicity. We show that topological complexity of random simplical complexes in the medial regime is bounded above by 2 and it equals 2 for a class of homogenous medial regime random simplicial complexes, a.a.s.

</details>

Abstract: We propose Choquet extension for set-pair functions, $L^p$ integration of Choquet extensions, and global extension constants, and apply these to investigate optimization problems and bound many combinatorial and geometric quantities. Our research line is also applicable to the study of original Choquet extension; within this framework, parallel results for the original version are obtained. Specifically, we use Choquet-type extensions to build an equivalent functional representation of set-based fractional optimization, which finds applications in various settings, such as maxcut, bipartiteness ratio, and conductance on graph limits or Riemannian manifolds. We further establish the $L^p$ integration of a family of Choquet extensions, and apply it to derive spectral bounds for conductance and other combinatorial quantities on measure spaces. A monotonicity inequality on global extension constants is proposed, which unifies classical estimates and uncovers new bounds for a lot of geometric and combinatorial quantities, such as torsional rigidity, Cheeger constants, Dirichlet $p$-isoperimetric constant, and $p$-Laplacian eigenvalues, in totally distinct underlying structures----including hypergraphs, graph limits, Riemannian manifolds and metric measure spaces.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zarankiewicz问题是极值图论中的经典难题，即求二部图中不含K_{s,t}子图的最大边数。对于小尺寸的Z(m,n,3,3)，此前已有部分结果，但存在相邻区域的边界未闭合或需要修正。作者旨在通过统一的证书化计算机辅助证明方法，填补Z(12,n,3,3)、Z(13,22,3,3)等有限切片的结果，并修正相邻的前沿值。

Method: 作者采用证书驱动（certificate-based）的计算机辅助证明策略。核心方法包括：(1) 通过轨道证书（orbit certificates）排除下一个边计数下的所有假设矩阵；(2) 利用删除引理（deletion lemmas）和显式见证（explicit witnesses）闭合相邻单元；(3) 对于13×22的情形，将证明约化为83个度分布（degree profiles），对其中77个进行有理分离，剩余6个通过标记行同余、枚举、模Gram检验和精确Farkas证书排除；(4) 所有声称的结论用标准库Python和精确整数/有理数运算重放验证，浮点优化仅用于发现证书。

Result: 得到的结果包括：Z(12,n,3,3)=6n（18≤n≤22），Z(13,22,3,3)=137，Z(13,18,3,3)=116，Z(14,18,3,3)=124，Z(15,18,3,3)=132，Z(14,17,3,3)=118，Z(15,17,3,3)=126，以及132≤Z(16,17,3,3)≤133。关键的承重上界来自12×18和13×18的精确证书包，而16×17条目因仅认证了132边下界和已发表的133上界而有意报告为区间。

Conclusion: 作者通过一个统一的、可验证的证书化证明框架，成功闭合了Z(12,n,3,3)等有限切片和多个相邻单元的结果，并修正了相邻前沿。该工作展示了精确计算机辅助证明与可重放验证相结合的方法论价值，同时强调所有结论均经过精确算术的完整重放，保证了证明的可靠性和可复现性。

Abstract: The Zarankiewicz number Z(m,n,s,t) is the maximum number of edges in a bipartite graph with parts of orders m and n containing no copy of Ks,t. We give one combined, certificate-based computer-assisted proof for two finite slices and a corrected neighboring frontier: Z(12,n,3,3) = 6n (18 <= n <= 22), Z(13,22,3,3) = 137, Z(13, 18, 3, 3) = 116, Z(14, 18, 3, 3) = 124, Z(15,18,3,3) = 132, Z(14, 17, 3, 3) = 118, Z(15, 17, 3, 3) = 126, 132 <= Z(16,17,3,3) <= 133. The load-bearing new upper bounds are the exact 12 x 18 and 13 x 18 certificate packages. Their orbit certificates exclude every hypothetical matrix at the next edge count. Deletion lemmas and explicit witnesses close four neighboring cells, while the 16 x 17 entry is deliberately reported as an interval because only its 132-edge lower witness and the published 133 upper bound are certified here. Separately, the 13 x 22 proof excludes 138 ones by reducing to 83 degree profiles, rationally separating 77 of them, and eliminating the remaining six by marked-row congruences, leave enumeration, modular Gram tests, and exact Farkas certificates. All accepted claims are replayed by standard-library Python and exact integer/rational arithmetic; floating-point optimization is used only to discover certificates.

</details>


### [73] [Sharp asymptotics for regular induced subgraphs of type-based random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08169)
*Ariel Edgardo Levy*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了图中最大正则导出子图的阶数问题。作者在type-based（图on）随机模型框架内，从上下两个方向确定了最优常数为sqrt(2e)≈2.33，证明了任何type-based模型几乎必然满足F(G)≥(sqrt(2e)-o(1))√n，同时构造出满足f(n)≤(sqrt(2e)+o(1))√n的例子。这意味着在该模型族内sqrt(2e)是最优的，进一步改进上界必须依赖非type-based的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős-Fajtlowicz-Staton猜想询问f(n)/log n是否趋于无穷。此前所有上界改进（Bollobás、Alon-Krivelevich-Sudakov、Dyson-McKay）均来自type-based随机模型，Dyson-McKay给出了f(n)≤√(163n/9)≈4.26√n。本文动机在于确定整个type-based模型族中的最优常数，以明确该方法的极限，并判断任何进一步突破是否需要全新的非type-based方法。

Method: 作者从上下界两个方向同时逼近：一方面构造type-based随机模型，证明存在图满足F(G)≤(√(2e)+o(1))√n，从而给出f(n)的上界；另一方面证明在任意type-based模型中，随机图几乎必然满足F(G)≥(√(2e)-o(1))√n的下界。两者结合即确定该模型族内的最优常数为√(2e)。

Result: 证明了f(n)≤(√(2e)+o(1))√n≈2.33√n，同时证明所有type-based模型渐近几乎必然满足F(G)≥(√(2e)-o(1))√n。因此√(2e)是该模型族内的最优常数（上下界一致），显著改进了Dyson-McKay的4.26√n结果。

Conclusion: type-based随机模型在上界估计方面的潜力已被完全挖掘，最优常数√(2e)≈2.33√n已被确定。若要进一步改进f(n)的上界，或证明Erdős-Fajtlowicz-Staton猜想（f(n)/log n→∞），必须采用非type-based的全新构造方法。这为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: For a graph G let F(G) denote the largest order of a regular induced subgraph of G, and let f(n) = min{F(G) : |V(G)| = n}. A problem of Erdos, Fajtlowicz and Staton asks whether f(n)/log n -> infinity. Every improvement on the upper bound for f has come from a type-based (graphon) random model: Bollobas, then Alon-Krivelevich-Sudakov, then Dyson-McKay, who obtained f(n) <= sqrt(163n/9) = 4.2557 sqrt(n). We determine the optimal constant for that entire family, from both sides. We show f(n) <= (sqrt(2e)+o(1)) sqrt(n) = 2.3316... sqrt(n), and that every type-based model satisfies F(G) >= (sqrt(2e)-o(1)) sqrt(n) asymptotically almost surely. Consequently the constant sqrt(2e) is optimal within the family, and any further improvement on the upper bound for f(n) -- in particular any counterexample to the Erdos-Fajtlowicz-Staton conjecture -- must come from a construction that is not type-based.

</details>


### [74] [Universal Hilbert series coefficients of the superspace coinvariant ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08187)
*Sylvie Corteel,John Lentfer*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了超空间（superspace）余不变环的Hilbert级数，其系数由钩形划分（hook-shaped partitions）索引。作者给出了这些系数的显式正组合解释及相关生成函数，并证明了u=-q^2处的特殊化系数为二项式系数之差，从而验证了Sagan–Swanson（2024）关于系数符号翻转回文性的猜想，同时推广到所有m≥1的u=-q^m特殊化并给出了闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超空间余不变环的Hilbert级数系数缺乏显式正组合解释，且Sagan–Swanson提出的系数具有"符号翻转回文性"（palindromic up to sign）的猜想尚未被证明。作者旨在提供这些系数的正组合解释并解决该猜想。

Method: 作者对Hilbert级数的系数（由钩形划分索引）给出了一个显式的正组合解释，并导出了相关生成函数。通过将该Hilbert级数在u=-q^2处特殊化，将系数表示为二项式系数之差，从而证明回文性（至符号翻转）。进一步对一般m≥1的u=-q^m特殊化建立闭式公式。

Result: 给出了Hilbert级数系数的正组合解释与生成函数；证明u=-q^2特殊化的系数是二项式系数之差，由此验证了Sagan–Swanson猜想（系数为符号翻转回文）；并将结果推广至任意正整数m，得到u=-q^m特殊化的闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为超空间余不变环的Hilbert级数提供了完整的正组合刻画，解决了Sagan–Swanson猜想，并建立了更一般的统一框架。

Abstract: The coefficients that determine the Hilbert series of the superspace coinvariant ring are indexed by hook-shaped partitions. We give a manifestly positive combinatorial interpretation of these coefficients, together with several generating functions for them. Specializing this Hilbert series at $u=-q^2$, we show that its coefficients are differences of binomial coefficients. Consequently, this proves a conjecture of Sagan--Swanson (2024) that these coefficients are palindromic up to sign. More generally, for every $m \geq 1$ we obtain closed-form expressions for the $u = -q^m$ specialization.

</details>


### [75] [Floridian Solitaire: A New Variant of Bulgarian Solitaire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08313)
*Aaron Meyerowitz,Stephen J. Curran,Stephen C. Locke,Richard M. Low*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究保加利亚接龙棋的一种带选择且限制在分离分区上的变体。作者证明当n>73时存在周期（即必胜初始位置），并通过计算确定存在周期的n的完整集合为{2,6,8,11,14,16,18,21}以及所有n≥23，其他n值均不存在周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典保加利亚接龙棋是一个无选择、无损失的一人游戏，其长期行为已被充分理解：每条轨迹最终进入周期，周期中的分区由二元向量和二元项链参数化。然而，绝大多数属于周期的分区并不满足"分离"性质（即不同部分大小之差至少为2）。这一事实促使作者研究一个有选择但限制在分离分区上的变体，以探索该限制下玩家的胜负情况。

Method: 作者将原始无选择游戏修改为有选择且允许失败的变体：玩家在每一步可以做出选择，但必须始终停留在分离分区上，如果无法做出合法移动则失败。作者通过数学证明（针对n>73的情形）结合对较小n值的计算枚举，系统分析该游戏中存在周期（即玩家能无限获胜）的充要条件。

Result: 证明了对于n>73，该变体游戏中存在周期，因此存在必胜初始位置。数值计算与主定理结合，确定存在周期的n的完整集合为：n∈{2,6,8,11,14,16,18,21}以及所有n≥23，其余n值均不存在周期。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了保加利亚接龙棋分离分区变体中存在周期的所有n值，证明了一个大n的渐近结果，并通过有限计算补全了小n的情形，最终给出了存在周期的n的精确分类。

Abstract: Bulgarian solitaire is a well-studied, no-choice, no-loss, one-player game involving stacks of cards. More formally, it is a self-map on the set of partitions of a fixed integer $n.$ As a finite dynamical system, its long-term behavior is well understood. Every trajectory ends in a cycle. The partitions that are in a cycle are parameterized by binary vectors, and the cycles by binary necklaces. Call a partition separated if distinct part sizes differ by at least two. The vast majority of partitions belonging to a cycle are not separated. Motivated by this fact, we consider a variant where the player has choices, but is restricted to separated partitions and, if unable to make a legal move, may lose. We prove that for $n>73$, there are cycles, and hence winning initial positions. We analyze the game for small values of $n$ and describe computations which, together with our main result, show that there are cycles for $n \in \{2,6,8,11,14,16,18,21\}$ and for $n \ge 23$, but for no other $n.$

</details>


### [76] [Posets of trek polynomials for directed trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08325)
*Marina Garrote-López,Nataliia Kushnerchuk,Liam Solus*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文刻画了由"trek"多项式（枚举有向树子图的生成多项式）定义的映射图像所对应的偏序集$P_\varphi$，并利用该刻画计算了代数簇$V_\varphi$的线性张成、证明其为环面簇（toric）并推导其消逝理想的基。此外，证明了trek多项式的偏序集是π-系统当且仅当有向树满足由Stanley P-分拆刻画的性质，且两个不同有向树对应的簇相交于严格低维的簇，解决了图模型统计中的结构可辨识性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当一个代数簇$V_\varphi$恰好是某个由组合生成多项式定义的多项式映射$\varphi$的像时，簇的几何性质可以反映生成多项式所满足的恒等式，从而将组合学与代数几何联系起来。作者希望利用这种联系来回答关于$V_\varphi$的问题，特别是通过一个由多项式系数向量定义的偏序集$P_\varphi$来实现。

Method: 本文对定义$\varphi$的生成多项式为有向树（treks）子图枚举多项式的情形，完整刻画了偏序集$P_\varphi$的结构。利用该刻画，作者计算了$V_\varphi$的线性张成，证明了簇的环面性质（toric），并推导出其消逝理想的基。同时，通过Stanley的P-partitions理论给出了偏序集为π-系统的充要条件，并利用簇的维数比较解决结构可辨识性问题。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对trek多项式完全刻画了偏序集$P_\varphi$；(2) 利用该刻画计算了$V_\varphi$的线性张成、证明了其为toric簇并给出了消逝理想的基；(3) 证明了trek多项式的偏序集为π-系统当且仅当对应的有向树满足由Stanley P-分拆刻画的性质；(4) 证明了两个不同有向树对应的簇相交于严格低维的簇，从而解决了图模型程序中的结构可辨识性问题实例。

Conclusion: 通过将组合对象（有向树子图）的生成多项式与代数几何簇$V_\varphi$的几何性质建立精确联系，本文不仅提供了$P_\varphi$的完整刻画及其在计算簇性质上的应用，还揭示了该问题与Stanley的P-partitions理论的深层关联，并成功将纯数学工具应用于统计学中图模型的结构可辨识性这一实际问题。

Abstract: When a variety $V_\varphi$ equals the image of a polynomial map $\varphi$ whose coordinate functions are combinatorial generating polynomials (i.e.~polynomials enumerating combinatorial objects), the geometry of $V_\varphi$ reflects identities satisfied by the generating polynomials. The resulting interplay between combinatorics and algebraic geometry can be used to answer questions about $V_\varphi$. A recent technique proposes to do so using a partially ordered set (poset) $P_\varphi$ defined via the coefficient vectors of the polynomials defining $\varphi$. This paper characterizes the poset $P_\varphi$ when the generating polynomials defining $\varphi$ enumerate subgraphs of a directed tree known as treks. The characterization is used to compute the linear span of $V_\varphi$, prove it is toric and deduce a basis for its vanishing ideal. It is also shown that this poset of trek polynomials for a directed tree is a so-called $π$-system if and only if the tree satisfies a property characterized via Stanley's P-partitions. As an additional consequence, it is shown that the varieties for two distinct directed trees intersect in a strictly lower-dimensional variety. This solves an instance of the structural identifiability problem in the graphical models program from statistics.

</details>


### [77] [Complement minimally non-totally unimodular matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08334)
*Suyoung Choi,Mathieu Vallée*

TL;DR: 本文证明了在行/列置换与补运算下，唯一的补极小非全幺模矩阵是循环矩阵C₃和C₅，解决了Chervet、Grappe和Vallée提出的猜想。作为推论，由全等模矩阵的行生成的单纯锥具有正则幺模Hilbert三角剖分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望解决Chervet、Grappe和Vallée提出的关于补极小非全幺模矩阵分类的猜想，这是组合矩阵论中的一个开放问题。

Result: 严格证明了补极小非全幺模矩阵（在行/列置换和补运算下）只有C₃和C₅两类，并由此推导出全等模矩阵生成的单纯锥存在正则幺模Hilbert三角剖分。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅解决了一个公开猜想，还给出了全等模矩阵在几何与组合优化中的一个重要推论，即相关单纯锥具有良好的三角剖分性质。

Abstract: We prove that, up to row and column permutations and complement operations, the only complement minimally non-totally unimodular matrices are the cycle matrices $C_3$ and $C_5$. This settles a conjecture of Chervet, Grappe, and Vallée. As a consequence, every simplicial cone generated by the rows of a totally equimodular matrix admits a regular unimodular Hilbert triangulation.

</details>


### [78] [The Volume Helly Theorem in the plane, colorful version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08367)
*Imre Bárány,Bobby Miraftab,Leonidas Theocharous*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维平面上的彩色体积Helly定理：存在常数V>0，使得如果四个有限凸集族F1-F4满足任意横截交集面积至少为V，则某族中所有凸集的交集面积至少为1。核心引理是关于四个面积至多1的凸四边形，各自可从中选取一个定义半平面，使这四个半平面的交集面积不超过4096。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Helly定理及其彩色变体在组合几何中具有重要地位，而体积版本的Helly定理（即考虑测度而非单纯交集非空）是近年来的活跃研究方向。作者希望将体积型Helly定理推广到彩色版本，即从多个族中选择凸集时，只需保证横截交集体积足够大，就能保证某一族整体的交集非空（且体积至少为1）。

Method: 证明分为两部分：(1) 核心引理：对四个面积至多1的凸四边形Q1,...,Q4（每个为4个半平面交集），证明每个Qi中存在一个定义半平面Hi，使得这4个半平面的交集面积不超过4096。(2) 利用该引理结合经典彩色Helly定理的思想和打包/体积论证，推导出体积彩色Helly定理。

Result: 证明了在R^2中存在常数V>0（具体量级由4096等常数导出），使得若四个有限凸集族的所有横截交集的Lebesgue测度至少为V，则至少存在一个族i使得该族所有凸集的交集测度至少为1。此外建立了相应的四边形半平面核心引理，给出常数4096的界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了二维彩色体积Helly定理，这是经典彩色Helly定理与体积Helly定理的结合推广。核心贡献在于发现了四边形与其定义半平面之间的体积控制关系，为更一般维数和高维彩色体积Helly问题的研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: We prove a colorful volume Helly theorem for convex sets in $\mathbb R^2$: There is a constant $V>0$ such that if $\mathfrak F_1,\mathfrak F_2,\mathfrak F_3,\mathfrak F_4$ are finite families of convex sets in $\mathbb R^2$ and if $|\bigcap_1^4F_i|\ge V$ for every transversal $F_i\in \mathfrak F_i,\; (i=1,\ldots,4)$, then $|\bigcap \mathfrak{F}_i|\ge 1$ for some $i$. Here $|A|$ is the Lebesgue measure of $A\subset \mathbb R^d$. The main ingredient is the following theorem. Let $Q_1,\ldots,Q_4\subset\mathbb R^2$ be convex quadrilaterals of area at most $1$, where of course each $Q_i$ is the intersection of 4 halfplanes. Then for every $Q_i$ there is one of these halfplanes $H_i$, say, such that $|\bigcap_1^4 H_i| \le 4096$.

</details>


### [79] [Optimal Finite Interval Discrepancy via Binary Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08431)
*Arthur F. Ramos,David B. Hulak,Ruy J. G. B. de Queiroz*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了de Bruijn–Erdős区间差异问题有限水平版本中lex-merge策略的最优性猜想：任何从单一正质量出发、通过二元细分直到n个质量的过程，在某一阶段最大与最小质量之比至少为2^{1-1/⌈n/2⌉}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeLeo等人提出了经典de Bruijn–Erdős区间差异问题的有限水平版本，构造了lex-merge策略并猜想其差异值2^{1-1/⌈n/2⌉}对每个n都是最优的。证明这个猜想是本文的动机。

Method: 作者将问题推广到任意正质量的二元细化过程，通过追踪细化过程中的最小质量，并利用"在过程中点附近一个片段的强制存活"这一关键观察来建立下界。证明还推广到了r元细化的情形。

Result: 证明了lex-merge策略差异值的最优性猜想，建立了任意二元细化过程最大/最小质量比的下界，并给出了对应r元细化的普适下界。

Conclusion: 该结果完全解决了DeLeo等人提出的猜想，将区间差异问题的最优下界推广到了更一般的正质量细化过程，并延伸至r元细化的情形。

Abstract: DeLeo, Henderschedt, and Wells introduced a finite-horizon version of the classical de Bruijn--Erdos interval discrepancy problem. Starting from the unit interval, one repeatedly splits an existing interval into two until $n$ intervals are present, and one minimizes the largest ratio between the longest and shortest intervals over all intermediate partitions. They constructed the lex-merge strategy, whose discrepancy is $2^{1-1/\lceil n/2\rceil}$, and conjectured that this value is optimal for every $n$. We prove the conjecture. More generally, we establish a sharp lower bound for arbitrary binary refinement processes of positive masses: any process that starts with one positive mass, repeatedly replaces one mass by two positive masses with the same total, and terminates with $n$ masses must at some stage have largest-to-smallest ratio at least $2^{1-1/\lceil n/2\rceil}$. The proof tracks the minimum mass under refinement and uses the forced survival of a piece near the midpoint of the process. We also record the corresponding universal lower bound for $r$-ary refinements.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $a$ and $b$ be distinct real weights. An $\{a,b\}$-edge-weighting of a tree assigns one of these weights to each edge and is proper if adjacent vertices have different sums of incident edge weights. For every such pair, we give an explicit structural characterization of the trees that do not admit a proper $\{a,b\}$-edge-weighting. If $ab(a+b)\neq0$, then $K_2$ is the only tree without such a weighting. If $a+b=0$, then a tree has no proper $\{a,b\}$-edge-weighting exactly when every vertex has degree $1$ or $3$ and the subgraph induced by the degree-$3$ vertices has a perfect matching. For the remaining case $ab=0$, form the spanning forest consisting of the edges whose deletion leaves two odd-order components. A tree $T$ has no proper $\{a,b\}$-edge-weighting exactly when both bipartition classes have odd order and every component of this forest satisfies two conditions. First, every component satisfies the preceding degree-and-matching condition. Second, within each component, the degree of a vertex $v$ in the forest plus twice the number of incident edges $e$ outside the forest for which the component of $T-e$ not containing $v$ has an odd number of vertices from each bipartition class is independent of $v$. For every fixed pair of distinct real weights, the proofs yield a linear-time algorithm that decides whether a proper $\{a,b\}$-edge-weighting exists and constructs one when it does.

</details>

Abstract: George E. Andrews (2016) introduced the Kimberling index, $K(π)$, of a partition $π$ of a positive integer $n$, which is defined as
  $$K(π) = (\text{largest part of } π) - (\text{least part of } π) - (\text{number of parts of } π).$$
  Based on Kimberling index, Andrews defined five partition functions, $K_>(n),$ $K_<(n),$ $K_\leq(n),$ $K_=(n),$ and $K_\geq(n)$, called Kimberling partition functions, which count the numbers of partitions of a positive integer $n$ for which the Kimberling index $K(π) $ is $>0$, $<0$, $\leq0$, $=0$ and $\geq 0$, respectively. He also gave the generating functions for $K_\le(n),$ $K_<(n),$ and $K_>(n)$ and established some relations connecting Kimberling partitions and other partition functions. Since then, the Kimberling partition functions and their generating functions remained unexplored. In this paper, we derive generating functions for $K_=(n),$ and $K_\geq(n)$, and establish some congruence relations of the five Kimberling partition functions by using the method of $q$-series identities.

</details>

Result: 确立了七个精确的有限Zarankiewicz值：z(12,18;3)=108, z(13,17;3)=110, z(13,18;3)=116, z(14,17;3)=118, z(14,18;3)=124, z(15,17;3)=126, z(15,18;3)=132。所有见证图构成一个由显式13×18矩阵生成的连通族，并提供了完整的补充材料，包括所有见证、可移植验证器、机器可读报告和完整性哈希。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的、计算机辅助的严格证明包，连同可验证的证书确保了七个Zarankiewicz值的正确性。所构造的见证图之间的连通关系（通过行/列删除和两种权重8行扩展）揭示了这些极值结构的内在联系。文中还清晰地区分了先前的数值计算成分与当前工作的贡献，提高了结果的可信度和可复现性。

Abstract: We present a unified proof and certificate package establishing seven exact finite Zarankiewicz values for the forbidden graph $K_{3,3}$: $z(12,18;3)=108$, $z(13,17;3)=110$, $z(13,18;3)=116$, $z(14,17;3)=118$, $z(14,18;3)=124$, $z(15,17;3)=126$, and $z(15,18;3)=132$.
  The witnesses form a connected family generated by an explicit $13 \times 18$ matrix with 116 ones. Deleting one row or one column and adding either of exactly two admissible weight-eight rows for this fixed labeled core produces the remaining witnesses. The exceptional upper-bound closure, $z(12,18;3)\leq108$, combines the published uniqueness of the extremal $12 \times 17$ graph with 103 edges and an exhaustive rejection of all $\binom{12}{6}=924$ possible degree-six column extensions. The supplement contains all witnesses, a portable verifier, a machine-readable report, and integrity hashes. Prior numerical ingredients and the role of the present work are separated cell by cell.

</details>

Abstract: Let $C_{2s}^{\mathrm{ad}}$ be the anti-directed cycle of length $2s$, where $s\geq2$. The minimum semidegree threshold is asymptotically tight. The proof develops Ramsey--Turán-type lattice-absorption lemmas with a transferral arising from the small-independence condition by virtue of a fork-type structure.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在深入理解图论中根密度的结构性质。此前Reiher和Schacht证明了边根三角形定理（edge-rooted triangle theorem），并提出团是否为2-强制的开放问题。本文的目标是推广这一结果到一般有限图F，回答其开放问题，并提供显式的稳定性估计。

Method: 证明分为两个核心步骤。第一步利用熵论证（entropy argument），将常数根密度条件转化为关于log W的加性恒等式；第二步采用Hoeffding分解（Hoeffding decomposition），推导该恒等式的所有可能的解。该方法具有统一性，可适用于多种结构（超核、Aldous-Hoover超图、有向核和锦标赛核）。

Result: 主要结果为：对于任意有限图F（至少含一条边）和图论W，若F在每个边上的根密度几乎处处为常数，则要么t(F,W)=0，要么W是常数。对于边传递图F，只需检查一个根方程即可。该结果恢复了Reiher和Schacht的边根三角形定理，并证明了每个团都是2-强制的。此外还给出了W有正下界时显式的稳定性估计，并将结果推广到四类更一般的核结构。

Conclusion: 本文在根密度与核结构之间建立了深刻联系，统一了已知的边根三角形定理并将其推广至一般图F，同时攻克了团为2-强制这一开放猜想。熵论证与Hoeffding分解的组合方法展现出普适性，可推广至超图、有向图及锦标赛等多种组合对象，为该领域后续研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a finite graph with at least one edge, and let $W$ be a graphon. We show that if the density of $F$ rooted at each edge is almost everywhere constant, then either $t(F,W)=0$ or $W$ is constant. For edge-transitive $F$, one rooted equation suffices. This recovers the edge-rooted triangle theorem of Reiher and Schacht. In their terminology, our result also shows that every clique is $2$-forcing, answering a question they posed. We give an explicit stability estimate when $W$ is bounded away from zero. Our proof has two steps: an entropy argument turns constant rooted densities into an additive identity for $\log W$, and a Hoeffding decomposition determines all solutions of that identity. The same method gives exact classifications and quantitative stability estimates for symmetric uniform hyperkernels, dissociated Aldous--Hoover hypergraphons, directed kernels, and tournamentons.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入加权bond格（weighted bond lattice），在其上定义新的图多项式不变量和对称函数不变量。利用字典序shellability（lexicographic shellability）理论证明弦图情形下对称函数最高次齐次分量的e-正性，进而推出多项式不变量的γ-正性。通过构造对称函数的结构来分析其性质并提出Schur对数凹性猜想。

Result: 证明了对于任意弦图，新的图多项式不变量是γ-正的；新对称函数不变量的最高次齐次分量对弦图是e-正的。新多项式不变量涵盖了Narayana多项式、树-Eulerian多项式和二项式-Eulerian多项式等经典例子。对称函数不变量可推广色多项式，包含Haiman的parking function对称函数等已知例子。

Conclusion: 加权bond格为图不变量提供了统一框架，将多项式的γ-正性和对称函数的e-正性联系起来。作者提出Schur对数凹性猜想，该猜想在色多项式特例下归结为Huh的对数凹性定理，表明这是一条有望统一色多项式与更广泛图不变量性质的新路径。

Abstract: The classical bond lattice of a graph was used in a formula of Whitney to compute the chromatic polynomial. In this paper, we use weighted versions of the bond lattice, to introduce and study a new polynomial and a new symmetric function invariant of a graph. Examples of the new polynomial invariant include the classical Narayana polynomials (for the path graph), the tree-Eulerian polynomials (for the complete graph), and the binomial-Eulerian polynomials (for the star graph). These examples suggest an interesting connection to $h$-polynomials of general graph-associahedra. We prove that for any chordal graph, our polynomial graph invariant is $γ$-positive.
  Multiweighted bond posets yield a symmetric function graph invariant that is an analog of the chromatic polynomial. The highest degree homogeneous component of this symmetric function is of particular interest. The parking function symmetric function introduced by Haiman arises as an example, as do symmetric functions studied by the first author in connection with multibracketed Lie algebras and with colored exterior algebras. The $γ$-positivity result mentioned above is a specialization of an $e$-positivity result for the highest degree homogeneous component, which we prove for any chordal graph using the theory of lexicographic shellability. We conjecture that this symmetric function is Schur-log-concave, which specializes to Huh's log-concavity theorem for the chromatic polynomial.

</details>


### [86] [An extremal theorem for graphs with non-negative Bakry--Émery curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09180)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chen, Liu 和 You 在 arXiv:2607.02297 中证明了正 Lin–Lu–Yau 曲率的极值定理，并提出了对其他离散曲率类似问题的猜想。本文旨在将该极值定理推广到非负 Bakry–Émery 曲率的设定下，特别使用非归一化拉普拉斯算子。

Method: 通过分析图的边数与 Bakry–Émery 曲率性质 CD(0,∞) 之间的关系，构造了边数的下界阈值（即二项式系数减去 floor(n/2) 再减 2），并证明了超过该边数阈值时所有图都满足 CD(0,∞) 条件。

Result: 证明了任意阶数 n ≥ 7 的图，只要其边数大于二项式系数减去 floor(n/2) 再减 2，则该图满足 CD(0,∞)（即非负 Bakry–Émery 曲率）；同时构造反例证明了该边数阈值是最优的，不可进一步改进。

Conclusion: 在非归一化拉普拉斯算子的 Bakry–Émery 曲率框架下，给出了一个锐利（sharp）的极值定理，为离散曲率极值问题提供了重要补充，且结果达到最优边界。

Abstract: Recently, Chen, Liu, and You (An extremal theorem for positive curvature of graphs, arXiv:2607.02297) proved an extremal theorem for positive Lin--Lu--Yau curvature. They further proposed the similar problems for other discrete curvature. In this paper, we prove a sharp extremal theorem for non-negative Bakry--Émery curvature with non-normalized Laplacian: every graph of order \(n\geq 7\) with more than \[
  \binom {n}{2}-\lfloor{\frac{n}{2}\rfloor}-2 \] edges satisfies $CD(0,\infty)$, and this threshold is optimal.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个广泛研究的问题：何时生成函数的Hankel变换成为(α,β) Somos-4序列，以及如何推导α和β。Wang-Zhang已建立了充分条件，但Barry在多个领域中提出的许多(α,β) Somos-4猜想仍未解决，需要新的理论工具加以攻克。

Method: (i) 在有理函数域上扩展Wang-Zhang充分条件，并与Sulanke-Xin二次变换结合；(ii) 证明Somos-4序列的奇偶子序列保持Somos-4结构但参数发生变换；(iii) 利用正交多项式理论证明Hankel行列式公式。

Result: 解决了Barry所有尚未解决的(α,β) Somos-4猜想；建立了Hurwitz变换猜想；证明了Hankel行列式公式及周期性Hankel行列式相关的若干猜想。

Conclusion: 通过有理函数域扩展和二次变换结合，以及正交多项式方法，完整解决了Barry提出的(α,β) Somos-4系列猜想，为该领域提供了统一的证明框架。

Abstract: An $(α,β)$ Somos-$4$ sequence $S_n$ is defined by the recurrence $S_nS_{n-4}=αS_{n-1}S_{n-3}+βS_{n-2}^2$ ($n\geq 4$), with suitable initial values, where $α$ and $β$ are constant parameters. A widely studied question is the following: When does the Hankel transform of a generating function become an $(α,β)$ Somos-4 sequence? In particular, how can $α$ and $β$ be derived for such a function? A sufficient condition for this problem has been established by Wang and Zhang. In this paper, we obtain the following three main results.
  (i): We extend the Wang--Zhang sufficient condition by working over the rational function field. Then we combine this result with the Sulanke--Xin quadratic transformation to resolve all of Barry's currently unsolved $(α,β)$ Somos-4 conjectures, which arise in diverse contexts, including generalized Catalan recurrences, Riordan arrays, generalized Bernstein arrays, and elliptic curves.
  (ii): We show that the odd and even subsequences of an $(α,β)$ Somos-4 sequence are again $(α,β)$ Somos-4 sequences with transformed parameters. This is employed to establish Barry's Hurwitz transform conjecture.
  (iii): Using the theory of orthogonal polynomials, we prove a Hankel determinant formula and thereby prove a conjecture related to the $(α,β)$ Somos-4 sequence. In addition, we prove some conjectures on formulas for periodic Hankel determinants.

</details>


### [88] [Diameter and Length of Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08716)
*Hee-Kap Ahn,Sergio Cabello,Otfried Cheong,Delia Garijo,Jeongwon Moon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了度量图的总长度上界为$(cyc(G) + max\{1, \ell(G)/2\}) \cdot diam(G)$，其中$\ell(G)$为叶子数，$cyc(G)$为圈数；该界是紧的，并刻画了取等号的度量图。作为应用，给出了由环或星通过固定数量点（两两或成组）的识别所得到的度量图直径的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量图是组合数学与几何分析中的重要研究对象，其总长度与直径之间的关系是度量图中核心的不等式问题。在已有针对树的类似结果（一棵树的总长度至多为$\max\{1, \ell/2\} \cdot diam$）的基础上，作者希望将这一结论推广到一般度量图（含圈的结构），并给出紧致上界及等号成立的刻画。

Method: 作者通过结合度量图的圈数（cyclomatic number）和叶子数来构造总长度的上界。通过分析度量图中从叶子出发的最长路径以及各基本圈对总长度的贡献，推导出一般度量图的长度-直径不等式。随后通过构造紧致示例并分析等号条件，对达到上界的度量图进行结构刻画。最后将该不等式应用于由环或星通过点识别（pairwise或成组）构造的度量图，导出直径的紧界。

Result: 得到了度量图总长度的紧致上界$L(G) \leq (cyc(G) + max\{1, \ell(G)/2\}) \cdot diam(G)$，证明了该界的紧性，并完整刻画了等号成立的度量图结构。同时，在环或星通过固定数量的点识别所得到的度量图中，给出了直径的紧致界。

Conclusion: 该结果将树情形的经典长度-直径不等式推广到一般度量图，提供了包含圈结构时总长度的最优上界，并通过等号刻画和紧界应用丰富了度量图的理论框架，对度量图设计、优化及相关几何组合问题具有参考意义。

Abstract: A metric graph is a metric space obtained from a finite collection of intervals whose endpoints are identified in groups. It can also be seen as a finite, edge-weighted graph where the continuum of points along the interior of each edge is taken into consideration, and each edge is locally isometric to an interval whose length is the edge-weight. The diameter of a metric graph $G$ is the maximum distance between all pairs of points of $G$. We show that the total length of a metric graph $G$ with $\ell(G)$ leaves, cyclomatic number $cyc(G)$, and diameter $diam(G)$ is at most $(cyc(G) + max\{1, \ell(G)/2\}) \cdot diam(G)$. Furthermore, we show that his bound is tight, and we characterize the metric graphs where equality holds. As an application, we provide tight bounds in certain cases for the diameter of metric graphs obtained from a cycle or a star by the identification of a fixed number of points (pairwise or in groups).

</details>


### [89] [A note on the partition function of a rectangle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08762)
*Krystian Gajdzica,Maciej Zakarczemny*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩形分拆函数p(n,n)和p(3,n)的渐近行为。p(n,n)计数n×n正方形分割为整数边矩形块的数目，p(3,n)计数3×n矩形的情形。主要结果给出了两者的渐近公式：p(n,n)≈exp((π/√3+o(1))n√(log n))，p(3,n)≈exp(π√(11n/3)+O(log n))，并附带给出更简单的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分拆函数在数论和组合学中具有重要地位，而矩形分拆（将矩形分割为整数边矩形块）作为一种自然推广，其渐近行为尚未被充分理解。研究p(n,n)和p(3,n)的渐近生长率有助于深入理解这类组合计数问题的结构性质。

Method: 通过解析组合学和分析数论的方法，对矩形分拆函数进行渐近估计。文章利用生成函数、指数级数求和以及精细的误差项分析，推导出主项。同时通过构造性方法分别建立上界和下界，并辅以独立的估计验证结果的正确性。

Result: 主要结果为：p(n,n)=exp((π/√3+o(1))n√(log n))，其主指数为n√(log n)量级，系数为π/√3；p(3,n)=exp(π√(11n/3)+O(log n))，主指数为√n量级，系数为π√(11/3)。此外还给出了更简洁的上下界估计。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了p(n,n)和p(3,n)的渐近生长速率，揭示了二者在指数增长阶上的本质差异（前者为n√(log n)阶，后者为√n阶），并提供了相应的上下界工具，为后续研究更一般矩形分拆函数奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate the asymptotic behavior of the rectangle partition functions $p(n,n)$ and $p(3,n)$. The function $p(n,n)$ counts partitions of the square $n\times n$ into rectangular blocks with integer sides, while $p(3,n)$ counts such partitions of the rectangle $3\times n$. Two rectangle partitions are identified when they contain the same multiset of rectangle types, and a block $a\times b$ is identified with a block $b\times a$. Our main results are $$ p(n,n) =
\exp\left( \left(\tfracπ{\sqrt3}+o(1)\right)n\sqrt{\log n} \right) $$ and $$ p(3,n) =
\exp\left( π\sqrt{\tfrac{11n}{3}}+O(\log n) \right). $$ We also present simpler upper and lower bounds for $p(n,n)$ and an independent upper bound for $p(3,n)$.

</details>


### [90] [Restricted generalized Schur numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08789)
*Collier Gaiser*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了拉姆齐理论中的一个广义Schur数问题：对于固定的ℓ≥2，证明了2-染色下使得任意2-着色必然存在一个具有恰好ℓ+1个不同整数解集的方程x₁+...+xₖ=xₖ₊₁的解的最小整数S₂(k;ℓ)，当k充分大时有精确公式S₂(k;ℓ)=k²+[(ℓ+1)(ℓ-2)/2+2]k+ℓ(ℓ-2)。特别地，当ℓ=2时，对所有k≥3有S₂(k;2)=k²+2k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在精确确定拉姆齐理论中广义Schur数的值。传统的Schur数研究关注单色解的存在性，而本文关注解的"型"——即解集中不同整数的个数。作者希望对于二元染色和固定参数ℓ，给出S₂(k;ℓ)的精确渐近公式，从而更精细地理解该方程的拉姆齐性质。

Method: 本文采用了组合构造（给出反例构造下界）与正则性论证/数论方法（证明上界）相结合的手段。通过构造k²+相关项长度的着色避免目标解来建立下界；同时利用ℓ≥2固定时的大k渐近分析，证明当n达到该下界时任何2-着色必然包含具有恰好ℓ+1个不同整数的单色解。

Result: 对固定ℓ≥2且k充分大时，证明了精确公式S₂(k;ℓ)=k²+[(ℓ+1)(ℓ-2)/2+2]k+ℓ(ℓ-2)。特别地，S₂(k;2)=k²+2k对所有k≥3成立。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了广义Schur数在二元着色情形下的精确闭式公式，丰富了拉姆齐理论中关于解的结构精细分类的研究，揭示了S₂(k;ℓ)作为k的二次多项式形式的精确规律。

Abstract: For $k\geq2$, let $S_r(k;\ell)$ be the smallest $n$, if exists, such that every $r$-coloring of $\{1,2,\ldots,n\}$ has a monochromatic solution $\mathcal{S}$ to the equation \[ x_1+x_2+\cdots+x_k=x_{k+1} \] such that the number of distinct integers in $\mathcal{S}$ is exactly $\ell+1$. We prove that, if $\ell\geq2$ is fixed, then \[ S_2(k;\ell)= k^2+\left[\frac{(\ell+1)(\ell-2)}{2}+2\right]k+\ell(\ell-2) \] for all large enough $k$. In particular, we have $S_2(k;2)=k^2+2k$ for all $k\geq3$.

</details>


### [91] [On two conjectures concerning Kemeny's constant of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08797)
*Wei Li,Wensheng Sun,Yujun Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文针对直径为二且阶数为n的连通图G，证明了任意两不同顶点间的有效电阻满足上界 r_G(u,v) ≤ (3+√5)(1/d_G(u)+1/d_G(v))，并由此推导出Kemeny常数满足 K(G) ≤ (3+√5)(n-1)，从而证实了Kim等人(2026)提出的K(G)=O(n)猜想。此外，当G与其补图均连通时，建立了Nordhaus-Gaddum型界 K(G)K(Ḡ)=O(n⁴)，证实了另一个相关猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kemeny常数是随机游走理论中衡量马尔可夫链混合收敛速度的重要参数，在图的谱理论中对应于非平凡拉普拉斯特征值倒数之和。对于直径为二的图，其Kemeny常数的渐近上界长期以来是一个悬而未决的问题。Kim等人(2026)提出了两个猜想：直径二的连通图满足K(G)=O(n)，以及图和补图均连通时满足K(G)K(Ḡ)=O(n⁴)。本文旨在通过有效电阻的精细估计来证明这两个猜想。

Method: 本文采用有效电阻（effective resistance）作为核心分析工具。首先，利用直径为二的图结构性质，即任意两顶点之间距离不超过2，对顶点间有效电阻建立了逐点估计 r_G(u,v) ≤ (3+√5)(1/d_G(u)+1/d_G(v))。随后，利用有效电阻与Kemeny常数之间的关系式（Kemeny常数可表示为所有顶点对间有效电阻与度积的加权和），将逐点有效电阻估计代入，推出K(G) ≤ (3+√5)(n-1)。对于第二个结论，结合G与补图Ḡ各自Kemeny常数的上界及补图中的度关系，推导出乘积形式的Nordhaus-Gaddum型界K(G)K(Ḡ)=O(n⁴)。

Result: (1) 证明了直径为二的连通图G中任意两顶点的有效电阻满足 r_G(u,v) ≤ (3+√5)(1/d_G(u)+1/d_G(v))；(2) 由此推出Kemeny常数满足 K(G) ≤ (3+√5)(n-1)，即K(G)=O(n)，证实了Kim等人的第一个猜想；(3) 进一步建立了Nordhaus-Gaddum型上界 K(G)K(Ḡ)=O(n⁴)，证实了Kim等人的第二个猜想。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入精确的有效电阻上界，彻底解决了Kim等人(2026)关于直径为二图的Kemeny常数线性上界及其与补图乘积阶的两个猜想。常数(3+√5)的系数估计较为精确，证明方法具有一般性，为进一步研究其他图类的Kemeny常数界提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected graph of order $n$ and diameter two. For any two distinct vertices $u,v\in V(G)$, we prove that $$ r_G(u,v)\leq (3+\sqrt{5})\left(\frac{1}{d_G(u)}+\frac{1}{d_G(v)}\right), $$ where $r_G(u,v)$ denotes the effective resistance between $u$ and $v$, and $d_G(u)$ denotes the degree of $u$ in $G$. Using this resistance estimate, we further prove that the Kemeny's constant of $G$, denoted by $\mathcal{K}(G)$, satisfies $$ \mathcal{K}(G)\leq (3+\sqrt{5})(n-1). $$ This confirms a conjecture of Kim et al. (2026) asserting that $\mathcal{K}(G)=O(n)$ for every connected graph of diameter two. We further establish the Nordhaus-Gaddum bound $\mathcal{K}(G)\mathcal{K}(\overline{G})=O(n^4)$ whenever both $G$ and $\overline{G}$ are connected, thereby confirming another conjecture of Kim et al. (2026), which states that $\mathcal{K}(G)\mathcal{K}(\overline{G})=O(n^4)$ whenever both graphs are connected.

</details>


### [92] [The $Δ$-Conjecture for CIS $d$-Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08799)
*Yinchen Liu,Quanyu Tang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Gurvich在1978年提出的Δ猜想：若完全图的边用d种颜色染色，且对每种颜色i的最大稳定集S_i的任意选择，这些稳定集的交集非空，则该染色不包含彩虹三角形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Δ猜想源自Gurvich 1978年的博士论文，是一个长期悬而未决的图论问题。证明该猜想有助于深入理解边染色完全图中彩虹三角形的结构特征以及稳定集与染色的深层联系。

Method: 论文给出了Δ猜想的严格数学证明。通过构造反证假设：假设存在彩虹三角形，利用最大稳定集的性质推导矛盾。具体地，在假设每个颜色类G_i的任意最大稳定集选择都有非空交集的前提下，证明无法同时存在三种颜色构成的两两不同颜色的三角形。

Result: 成功证明了Δ猜想，即上述关于最大稳定集交集非空的条件是边染色完全图不含彩虹三角形的充分条件。

Conclusion: Δ猜想得到解决，建立了边染色完全图中"任意最大稳定集选择的交集非空"与"无彩虹三角形"之间的等价性（在此条件方向上），推进了极值图论和染色理论中相关问题的理解。

Abstract: We prove the $Δ$-conjecture, which dates back to Gurvich's 1978 thesis. Specifically, let the edges of a complete graph be colored with colors $1,\ldots,d$, and for each $i$ let $G_i$ be the graph formed by the edges of color $i$. We prove that if every choice of a maximal stable set $S_i$ of $G_i$, one for each $i\in[d]$, has nonempty intersection, then the coloring contains no rainbow triangle.

</details>


### [93] [Asymmetric Catalan and Shi Hyperplane Arrangements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08834)
*Sucharita Biswas,Shubhanshu Prasad,Shushma Rani*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了Catalan和Shi排列的非对称扩展的区域问题，通过有限域方法确定了特征多项式并获得了区域数显式公式，同时引入两类新的组合对象（c-标记Dyck路径和c-构建函数），证明了它们分别与非对称Catalan排列区域和非对称Shi排列区域自然双射，解决了一个2022年Oberwolfach研讨会提出的问题，并将Stanley的经典对应关系进行了推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多重排列（如Catalan和Shi排列）非对称扩展的区域计数问题，并解决Theo Douvropoulos在2022年Oberwolfach枚举组合学研讨会上提出的问题。

Method: 使用有限域方法确定特征多项式以获得区域数公式；引入c-标记Dyck路径和c-构建函数两种新组合对象，构造与区域之间的自然双射。

Result: 获得了非对称Catalan和Shi排列区域数的显式公式；证明了区域与c-标记Dyck路径及c-构建函数之间存在自然双射。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了Stanley关于m-停车函数与m-Shi排列区域的经典对应关系，引入了全新的组合模型和独立的证明技术。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the regions of asymmetric extensions of the Catalan and Shi arrangements. We first determine the characteristic polynomials of a general class of Catalan and Shi arrangements using the finite field method, thereby obtaining explicit formulas for the number of regions. We then introduce two new classes of combinatorial objects: $\mathbf{c}$-labelled Dyck paths and $\mathbf{c}$-building functions, which generalize classical Dyck paths and parking functions, respectively. We prove that the regions of the asymmetric Catalan arrangement are in natural bijection with $\mathbf{c}$-labelled Dyck paths, while those of the asymmetric Shi arrangement are in natural bijection with $\mathbf{c}$-building functions. This resolves a problem proposed by Theo Douvropoulos during the 2022 Oberwolfach Workshop on Enumerative Combinatorics. In particular, our results extend Stanley's celebrated correspondence between $m$-parking functions and the regions of the $m$-Shi arrangement by introducing new combinatorial models and developing entirely different proof techniques.

</details>


### [94] [On degree powers in the degenerate Turán problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08838)
*Ping Hu,Ting Lan,Henry Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图G的p次度幂和e_p(G)的极值问题。作者基于Gao等人最近的工作，得到了关于ex_p(n, F)的稳定性结果：当n充分大时，所有极值图必须包含完全二部图K_{τ(F)-1, n-τ(F)+1}。该结果统一推广了此前关于二部图F的所有已知的ex_p(n,F)精确结果，并为偶圈、完全二部图、离散超立方体、毛虫森林和蜘蛛森林等图类给出了新的精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caro和Yuster在2000年提出了一个Turán型问题：对给定图F，确定ex_p(n,F)的最大值并刻画极值图。该问题对p次度幂和图论中的极值问题有重要意义，但此前仅有零散的已知结果，缺乏系统性的统一方法和稳定性刻画。

Method: 基于Gao, Liu, Ma和Pikhurko最近发展的方法（即利用退化图族的Turán数与独立顶点覆盖的关联），作者推广其技术路线，建立了适用于更广泛图类的稳定性分析框架，从而证明当n充分大时极值图必然含有完全二部图K_{τ(F)-1, n-τ(F)+1}作为子图。

Result: 得到了ex_p(n, F)的稳定性定理，统一推导出所有此前已知的二部图F的精确结果；并为偶圈、完全二部图、离散超立方体、毛虫森林和蜘蛛森林等具体图族给出了新的精确极值结果。

Conclusion: 该稳定性结果不仅统一了此前的分散结论，还拓展了ex_p(n,F)精确极值问题的适用范围，为后续研究提供了强有力的工具。

Abstract: Given a graph $G$ with degree sequence $d_{1},\ldots,d_{n}$ and a positive real number $p$, let $e_{p}(G)=\sum_{i=1}^{n} d_{i}^{p}$. For a fixed family of graphs $\mathcal F$, let $ex_{p}(n, \mathcal F)$ denote the maximum value of $e_{p}(G)$ over all $\mathcal F$-free graphs $G$ on $n$ vertices. In 2000, Caro and Yuster introduced the following Turán-type problem: For a positive integer $p$ and a fixed graph $F$, determine $ex_{p}(n, F)$, and characterize the extremal graphs $G$ on $n$ vertices that attain $ex_p(n, F)$. Recently, Gao, Liu, Ma and Pikhurko proved that $ex_{p}(n, \mathcal F)=(τ(\mathcal F)-1+o(1))n^p$ for real $p>\frac{1}{1-α}$, where $\mathcal F$ is a degenerate family of graphs with classical Turán number $ex(n, \mathcal F)=O(n^{1+α})$ for some $α\in[0,1)$, and $τ(\mathcal F)$ is the minimum size of an independent vertex cover over all bipartite graphs $F\in\mathcal F$. Based on their method, we obtain a stability result for $ex_{p}(n, \mathcal F)$, and prove that all extremal graphs must contain the complete bipartite graph $K_{τ(\mathcal F)-1,n-τ(\mathcal F)+1}$ when $n$ is sufficiently large. Our results can be used to deduce all previously known results about $ex_{p}(n, F)$ when $F$ is a bipartite graph and $n$ is sufficiently large. We also obtain several new exact results for $ex_{p}(n, F)$, namely, when $F$ is an even cycle, a complete bipartite graph, a discrete hypercube, a caterpillar forest, and a spider forest.

</details>


### [95] [All Polyominoes are $C_4$-face-magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08914)
*Parikshit Chalise,Richard M. Low,Arman Eisenkolb-Vaithyanathan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了所有多联骨牌（polyominoes）都是C₄-面幻图（C₄-face-magic），即存在一种顶点标号方式，使得每个四边形面（即单位正方形）的顶点标号之和为常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平面图的"面幻"性质，即能否通过顶点标号使所有同构于C_n的面具有相等的顶点和。这是一个图论与组合标号领域的自然问题，特别关注C₄-面幻图这一特殊情形。

Method: 通过构造性的标号方法，针对任意多联骨牌结构，设计一种从顶点集到{1,2,...,|V|}-的\uffffffff双射，使得每一个单位正方形面（即C₄面）的四个顶点标号之和为同一常数c，从而证明所有多联骨牌都是C₄-面幻图。

Result: 证明了对任意嵌入平面中的多联骨牌图，均存在满足C₄-面幻条件的顶点标号，即所有多联骨牌都是C₄-面幻图。

Conclusion: 所有多联骨牌均具有C₄-面幻性质，为平面图的面幻标号理论提供了新的结果。

Abstract: For a planar graph $G = (V, E)$ embedded in $\mathbb{R}^2$, let $\mathcal{F}(G)$ denote the set of faces of $G$. Then $G$ is called a \textit{$C_n$-face-magic} graph if there exists a bijection $f: V(G) \to \{1, 2, \dots, |V(G)|\}$ such that for any $F \in \mathcal{F}(G)$ with $F \cong C_n$, the sum of all the vertex labels along $C_n$ is a constant $c$. In this paper, we prove that all polyominoes are $C_4$-face-magic.

</details>


### [96] [The Maximum of $\operatorname{per}(I-A)$ in Odd Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08933)
*Yair Lavi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完整证明了Kim和Roush在1981年提出的关于$2k+1$阶双随机矩阵$I-A$的积和式的猜想，并完成了等式成立情形的完全分类，即极值矩阵恰好是$A_\star$的同步置换共轭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kim和Roush于1981年猜测，对于$n=2k+1>1$阶双随机矩阵$A$，$\operatorname{per}(I-A)$的最大值为$3\cdot 2^{k-2}$，并提出了一种块构造$A_\star$作为候选极值矩阵，但未证明该猜想，也未讨论唯一性问题。该猜想自提出以来长期未获解决。

Method: 作者对Kim-Roush猜想给出了完整的证明。证明方法围绕积和式的组合与矩阵结构分析展开，对$A_\star$的构造（$3\times3$全零对角块加上若干$2\times2$置换矩阵块）进行极值性论证，并通过分析等式条件来分类所有达到最大值的矩阵。

Result: 论文完全证明了$\max_{A\in\Omega_{2k+1}}\operatorname{per}(I-A)=3\cdot 2^{k-2}$，并进一步完成了等式情形的分类：所有达到最大值的矩阵恰好是$A_\star$的同步置换共轭（即同时对行列施加相同置换得到的矩阵）。

Conclusion: 本文不仅解决了1981年以来的Kim-Roush猜想，还额外确定了极值矩阵的唯一性（在同步置换共轭意义下），给出了该组合优化问题的完全刻画。

Abstract: Let $Ω_n$ denote the set of $n\times n$ doubly stochastic matrices. Kim and Roush conjectured in 1981 that, for $n=2k+1>1$, $
  \max_{A\inΩ_{2k+1}}\operatorname{per}(I-A)=3\cdot 2^{k-2}$. They proposed the block construction $A_\star=\frac12(J_3-I_3)\oplus
  P_2^{\oplus(k-1)}$, where $P_2=\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\1&0\end{pmatrix}$. Here $J_3$ is the $3\times3$ all-ones matrix. They did not claim
  uniqueness. We fully prove their conjecture and classify equality: the maximizers are exactly the simultaneous-permutation conjugates of
  $A_\star$.

</details>


### [97] [A proof of the cyclotomic conjecture and the non-existence of almost Moore digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08997)
*Jaskaran Kaur,Hitesh Kumar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个名为"分圆多项式猜想"（cyclotomic conjecture）的数学猜想，该猜想由Gimbert于1999年提出，精确描述了多项式F_{n,k}(x)=Φ_n(1+x+...+x^k)在有理数域上不可约的充要条件。作为该猜想成立的一个直接推论，本文证明了对于任何最大出度d>1且直径k>2的情况，不存在几乎摩尔有向图（almost Moore digraphs），从而解决了有向度-直径问题中一个长期悬而未决的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于有向度-直径问题（directed degree-diameter problem）中一个著名的开放问题——几乎所有摩尔有向图的存在性问题。Gimbert在1999年提出了分圆多项式猜想，而Conde等人（2014）证明了该猜想若成立，将蕴含几乎摩尔有向图的不存在性。因此，证明该分圆多项式猜想是解决几乎摩尔有向图存在性问题的关键路径，而本文正是沿着这条路径完成了最终证明。

Method: 本文通过数学分析方法证明了分圆多项式猜想。具体而言，针对n>2且k>1的多项式F_{n,k}(x)=Φ_n(1+x+...+x^k)，作者系统地研究了其不可约性条件，依据n和k的具体取值给出了精确的判定标准，从而完整地验证了Gimbert所提出的猜想。

Result: 本文证明了分圆多项式猜想成立，即F_{n,k}(x)在有理数域上的不可约性完全由n和k的数值关系决定。作为该结果的直接推论，文章证明了对于任何最大出度d>1和直径k>2，不存在几乎摩尔有向图。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是完成了分圆多项式猜想的证明，进而彻底解决了几乎摩尔有向图在d>1且k>2情况下不存在的问题。这不仅验证了Gimbert猜想的正确性，也标志着有向度-直径问题中一个长期未决的关键情形得到了最终答案。

Abstract: For $n>2$ and $k>1$, define the polynomial \[F_{n,k}(x) = Φ_n(1 + x + \cdots + x^k),\] where $Φ_n$ denotes the $n$-th cyclotomic polynomial. The \emph{cyclotomic conjecture} proposed by Gimbert (1999) exactly describes the irreducibility of $F_{n,k}(x)$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ in terms of $n$ and $k$. Conde, Gimbert, González, Miller and Miret (2014) established that the cyclotomic conjecture, if true, would imply the non-existence of almost Moore digraphs - a well-known open question concerning the directed degree-diameter problem. In this article, we prove the cyclotomic conjecture and, as a consequence, show that there are no almost Moore digraphs with maximum out-degree $d$ and diameter $k$ for any $d>1$ and $k>2$.

</details>


### [98] [$q$-Difference Equations for two classes of Pattern Avoiding Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09078)
*Paul Zinn-Justin*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究两个嵌套排列类Av(4123,4231,4312)⊂Av(4123,4312)的生成函数D(2)(z)和D(3)(z)，将它们与q-差分方程联系起来，通过求解这些方程证明生成函数不是D有限的，并给出系数的渐近增长率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究嵌套排列类的生成函数性质，特别是判断其是否为D有限的（D-finite），以及获取系数渐近行为，这对于理解排列组合结构具有重要意义。

Method: 将两个排列类的生成函数D(2)(z)和D(3)(z)与q-差分方程建立联系，通过求解这些q-差分方程来分析生成函数的性质，从而导出其非D有限性的结论和系数的渐近展开。

Result: 证明两个生成函数都不是D有限的；给出精确的渐近结果：D(3)(z)的第n项系数约为0.0189672325071988…×(4.46840899160393…)^n，D(2)(z)的第n项系数约为0.0849833632890843…×(3+2√2)^n×n^(-3/2)。

Conclusion: 嵌套排列类的生成函数可以通过q-差分方程来刻画，这类方程的解往往不是D有限的，但其系数仍能获得精确的渐近表达式，体现了排列类生成函数分析的深度与细致性。

Abstract: We study the nested permutation classes $Av(4123,4231,4312)\subset Av(4123,4312)$. We relate the corresponding generating functions $D^{(2)}(z)$, $D^{(3)}(z)$ to $q$-difference equations. Solving these equations allows to show that the generation functions are not D-finite, and that $[z^n]D^{(3)}(z)\sim 0.0189672325071988\ldots\, (4.46840899160393\ldots)^n$, $[z^n]D^{(2)}(z)\sim0.0849833632890843\ldots\, (3+2\sqrt2)^n n^{-3/2}$.

</details>


### [99] [Exact Periodicity, Surjectivity, and a Haar Limit Law for a Restarting Josephus Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09092)
*Lizhong Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究一种"重启式约瑟夫问题"（restarting Josephus process），其中每次删除后计数从当前最左幸存者重新开始。通过逆插入得到递推式F_k(q)=F_{k-1}(q)+1_{q mod k < F_{k-1}(q)}。作者建立了三项主要结果：(1) F_n的完全周期群恰为L_nℤ；(2) F_n满射到{1,...,n}（构造性、无条件但计算机辅助证明）；(3) 若Q̃_n在模L_n下均匀分布，则(F_n(Q̃_n)-1)/(n-1)收敛于[0,1]上的对称非退化极限分布，且具有几乎必然与L^r收敛（1≤r<∞）以及W_1距离下O(n^{-1/4})的收敛速度。此外还排除了对称beta律作为极限，并讨论了端点主导性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫问题（Josephus problem）是组合数学与数论中的经典问题。本文研究其变体——重启式约瑟夫过程，该变体具有独特的"全周期群"结构和与均匀分布对齐的极限行为。研究该问题的动机在于：(1) 理解该递归系统在模结构下的周期性质；(2) 证明满射性以刻画F_n的像集；(3) 建立均匀随机输入下极限分布的收敛性，揭示其与标准beta律等经典分布的区别。这些结果对理解离散递推系统的统计性质具有理论意义。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 逆向插入法（reverse insertion）推导出递推关系；(2) 中国剩余定理构造与显式素数估计相结合，将满射性证明归结为有限精确验证（计算机辅助）；(3) 使用公共Haar耦合（common Haar coupling）证明几乎必然收敛和L^r收敛，并推导W_1距离下O(n^{-1/4})的界；(4) 通过对数尺度边界质量估计排除对称beta律；(5) 对端点主导性问题进行枚举验证（n≤49）及素数级排除分析。

Result: 三项主要成果：(1) 完全周期群为L_nℤ，刻画了F_n的周期性结构。(2) F_n满射到{1,...,n}，证明是无条件的构造性证明，借助计算机完成有限证书验证。(3) 归一化的幸存位置(F_n(Q̃_n)-1)/(n-1)在均匀随机输入下收敛到一个对称、非退化的极限分布（非任何对称beta律），且具有几乎必然收敛和所有阶矩收敛，W_1距离收敛速度为O(n^{-1/4})。另外证明了端点严格主导两个最近内部位置（n≥4），排除了素数级作为最小反例，并精确验证到n=49。

Conclusion: 本文确立了重启式约瑟夫过程的完整周期群刻画、满射性以及极限分布的存在性和非beta性质，为该问题建立了坚实的理论基础。端点主导性作为未解问题被明确提出，并提供了部分进展（严格主导最近内部位置、排除素数级反例、n≤49验证）。这为后续研究极限分布的具体形式以及端点主导性的最终解决指明了方向。

Abstract: We study a restarting Josephus process in which the participants retain their linear order and counting restarts at the current leftmost survivor after every deletion. For step size $m$, put $q=m-1$, and let $F_n(q)$ denote the initial position of the survivor. Reverse insertion gives $F_1(q)=1$ and $F_k(q)=F_{k-1}(q)+\mathbf{1}_{\{q\bmod k<F_{k-1}(q)\}}$. Writing $L_n=\operatorname{lcm}(1,\ldots,n)$, we establish three results for the compatible residue system in this recurrence. First, the full period group of $F_n$ is exactly $L_n\mathbb{Z}$. Second, $F_n$ is surjective onto $\{1,\ldots,n\}$. The proof is constructive and unconditional but computer-assisted: a Chinese-remainder construction and explicit prime estimates reduce it to a finite exact certificate. Third, if $\widetilde Q_n$ is uniform modulo $L_n$, then $(F_n(\widetilde Q_n)-1)/(n-1)$ converges to a symmetric, nondegenerate law on $[0,1]$. A common Haar coupling yields almost-sure and $L^r$ convergence for every $1\le r<\infty$, together with an $O(n^{-1/4})$ bound in $W_1$. Logarithmic boundary-mass estimates rule out every symmetric beta law. We also formulate endpoint dominance as an open problem, prove strict dominance over the two nearest internal positions for every $n\ge4$, exclude prime levels as minimal counterexamples, and verify the claim exactly through $n=49$.

</details>


### [100] [A Proof of the Imbalance Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09191)
*Yousof Yavari*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Kozerenko和Skochko关于图边不平衡多重集为图列（graphic sequence）的猜想：当每条边的不平衡度均为正时，不平衡多重集是图列的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kozerenko和Skochko提出了一个猜想，指出若有限简单图G中每条边的不平衡度（两端点度数之差绝对值）均为正，则由所有边的不平衡度构成的多重集M_G是图列（即可以作为某个简单图的度序列）。本文旨在证明该猜想。

Method: 证明的核心工具是一个截断和的下界估计：当至少有k条边的不平衡度至少为k时，给出∑_{e∈E(G)} min{k, imb_G(e)}的下界。该下界可用于验证非递增的边不平衡度序列满足所有Erdős–Gallai不等式，再结合奇偶性论证，即可完成证明。

Result: 成功证明了Kozerenko和Skochko的猜想，即边不平衡度全为正时，不平衡多重集是图列。

Conclusion: 通过截断和的下界估计建立Erdős–Gallai不等式的验证，并辅以奇偶性计算，完整证明了该猜想。

Abstract: For an edge $uv$ of a finite simple graph $G$, its imbalance is $|d_G(u)-d_G(v)|$, and the imbalance multiset $M_G$ consists of the imbalances of all edges of $G$. Kozerenko and Skochko conjectured that $M_G$ is graphic whenever every edge has positive imbalance. We prove this conjecture. The main ingredient is a lower bound for the truncated sum $\sum_{e\in E(G)}\min\{k,\operatorname{imb}_G(e)\}$ when at least $k$ edges have imbalance at least $k$. This bound yields all Erdos-Gallai inequalities for the nonincreasing list of edge imbalances; a parity computation then completes the proof.

</details>


### [101] [The Sharp Upper Bounds for the Median Eigenvalues of Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09353)
*Zhengbo Chen,Yuzhenni Wang,Xiao-Dong Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了简单图G的HL-指数R(G)的上界。证明了对于K4-minor-free图或K2,3-minor-free图，有R(G)≤√5-1，且该上界可由无限族外平面图达到；同时证明了最大度至多d、平均度至多(d-2)(d²-2d+2)/(d²-3d+5)的三角形-free图满足R(G)≤√(d-2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-指数是图论中反映图特征值分布的重要参数，研究其在特定图类上的上界有助于理解图的结构性质与谱性质之间的关系。本文旨在为K4-minor-free图、K2,3-minor-free图以及某些度约束下的三角形-free图建立HL-指数的新上界。

Method: 利用图的minor-free性质、外平面图结构以及度约束条件，结合特征值估计和谱图论中的分析技巧，对HL-指数进行逐类推导并构造达到上界的无限族图。

Result: (1) 对K4-minor-free或K2,3-minor-free图，证明R(G)≤√5-1，且上界由无限族外平面图达到；(2) 对最大度≤d且平均度满足给定条件的三角形-free图，证明R(G)≤√(d-2)。

Conclusion: 本文将HL-指数上界的研究推广到了minor-free图和带度约束的三角形-free图上，得到的上界是紧的（至少对第一类），并通过构造性例子证实了其最优性。

Abstract: Let $λ_1\geqλ_2\geq\cdots\geqλ_n$ be the eigenvalues of a simple graph $G$ of order $n$. The HL-index of $G$ is defined by $R(G)=\max\|λ_h|,|λ_\ell|\}$ with $h=\lfloor(n+1)/2\rfloor$ and $\ell=\lceil(n+1)/2\rceil$.In this paper, we prove that if $G$ is $ K_4$-minor-free or $ K _ {2,3} $-minor-free, then $R(G)\leq\sqrt{5}-1$ with equality attained by an infinite family of outerplanar graphs.Moreover, we show that $R(G)\leq\sqrt{d-2}$ for triangle-free graphs with maximum degree at most $d$ and average degree at most $(d-2)(d^2-2d+2)/(d^2-3d+5)$.

</details>


### [102] [A sharp extension of Halin's removable-edge theorem to matchings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09394)
*Hojin Chu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了每个k-连通图G（满足δ(G)≥max{k+1,2m-2}）包含大小为m的k-可删匹配，除非G同构于K_{2m-1}，或(k,m)=(1,2)且G为圈。这证实了Li、Zhou、Fujita和Mao的猜想，且最小度界是紧的，两个例外情形不可避免。作为推论，max{k+1,2m-1}是无例外情形下保证此类匹配存在的最小度阈值。证明结合了Halin可删边定理的指定顶点集加强版与最大k-可删匹配的极值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Halin证明了每个满足δ(G)≥k+1的k-连通图G存在一条k-可删边。本文旨在将此结果从单条边推广到任意指定大小的匹配，探索在更大的最小度条件下是否可以保证任意大小的k-可删匹配存在。

Method: 证明方法包含两个主要部分：(1) 建立Halin可删边定理的一个"指定顶点集"加强版本；(2) 对最大k-可删匹配进行极值分析，通过分析最大k-可删匹配的结构性质并结合指定顶点集的加强定理，推导出所要求的匹配存在性或得到例外情形。

Result: 证明了对任意正整数k和m，满足δ(G)≥max{k+1,2m-2}的k-连通图G含有大小为m的k-可删匹配，除非G≅K_{2m-1}或(k,m)=(1,2)且G为圈。这证实了Li等人提出的猜想，且最小度界和例外情形均为最优、不可避免的。

Conclusion: 本文首次将Halin的可删边定理推广到任意大小的匹配，给出了无例外情形下保证k-可删匹配存在的紧的最小度阈值max{k+1,2m-1}。这一结果不仅证实了已有的猜想，还通过极值分析揭示了k-可删匹配结构中的关键性质，为后续相关问题提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: A subgraph $H$ of a $k$-connected graph $G$ is called \emph{$k$-removable} if $G-E(H)$ remains $k$-connected. Halin proved that every $k$-connected graph $G$ with $δ(G)\ge k+1$ has a $k$-removable edge. We extend this result from a single edge to matchings of any prescribed size by showing that, for positive integers $k$ and $m$, every $k$-connected graph $G$ with $δ(G)\ge\max\{k+1,2m-2\}$ contains a $k$-removable matching of size $m$, unless $G\cong K_{2m-1}$, or $(k,m)=(1,2)$ and $G$ is a cycle. This confirms a conjecture of Li, Zhou, Fujita, and Mao. The minimum degree bound is sharp, and both exceptions are unavoidable. Consequently, $\max\{k+1,2m-1\}$ is the sharp minimum degree threshold guaranteeing such a matching without exceptions. The proof combines a prescribed-set strengthening of Halin's removable-edge theorem with an extremal analysis of maximum $k$-removable matchings.

</details>


### [103] [Row pathwidth of complete binary trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09495)
*Jędrzej Hodor,Piotr Micek*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明：若高度为h的完全二叉树同构于某图H与一条路径的强乘积的子图，则H的路径宽度（pathwidth）为Ω(h)，解决了Bose等人在2022年提出的公开问题。该证明由OpenAI的GPT-5.6 Sol Pro发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
